# Aesthetic Relighting — ссылки на самостоятельные задачи

Снимок промптов из `docs/rend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md`, редакция 2, 2026-11-10. Это очередь запуска, а не разрешение выполнить все задачи подряд.

**Возможности ссылок:** `codex://threads/new` создаёт новый локальный чат и заполняет поле ввода. Ссылка не является fork этого чата и не содержит его идентификатор или историю. Все ссылки задают рабочую папку `C:/Users/Keyten/Desktop/projects/OpenJK-rend2`. Промпт нужно отправить вручную. Отдельное окно и автоматическая отправка документированными параметрами не обеспечиваются.

Официальный формат: [Commands — Deep links](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/app/commands#deep-links).

**Точность:** `prompt` содержит всё тело соответствующего блока `text` из плана, без ограждений Markdown, включая исходные переводы строк и завершающий перевод строки. Текст не сокращён и не дополнен. Обратное декодирование каждой ссылки проверено на побайтовое совпадение с UTF-8 исходником.

SHA-256 исходного плана: `6be406c0d077105a24e4e1bd18d0d34761e0c00fb389190a54d35f3218fa21b1`.

**Порядок:** сначала AR-34 → AR-35 → AR-36. Дальнейшие задачи разрешены только актуальным verdict и текущими решениями. AR-08 и AR-10 выполняются по необходимости; AR-38/39/40 — только если соответствующую ветвь выбрала AR-37. AR-37 можно выполнить раньше после AR-34; здесь она поставлена перед работой со всем корпусом, чтобы не задерживать доступный пилот. AR-27/28 стоят перед AR-26, поскольку контракт и replay нужны для приёмки первой карты.

**Изменения плана:** управленческие задачи могут менять направление, промпты и зависимости. После такого решения остановиться и пересоздать ссылки на изменённые задания из актуального плана; эти ссылки — точный снимок, а не автоматически обновляемые указатели. AR-16 архивирована и намеренно не имеет ссылки запуска.

[AR-34](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-34%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Decision%20%2F%20Experiment%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20S%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Rebase%20viability%20on%20ASR24%2F25%20and%20freeze%20a%20minimal%20proof%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20the%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%2C%20concurrent%20and%20staged%20changes%2C%20including%20ASR25.%20Verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20actual%20lamps%2Fscreens%2FBSP%2Fmodel%20emission%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20an%20independently%20motivated%20probable%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20Historical%20type%2C%20XYZ%2C%20power%20and%20exactLight%20certification%20are%20not%20aesthetic%20eligibility%20requirements.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0APreserve%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20meanings.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%20and%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Record%20modern-model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ARecognize%20the%20existing%20conditional%20stock%20successes%20and%20choose%20the%20smallest%20independent%20multi-fixture%20experiment%20that%20can%20justify%20or%20reject%20further%20investment%20in%20the%20current%20aesthetic%20reconstruction%20approach.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0ARead%20docs%2Frend2-stock-aesthetic-asr24.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-stock-discovery-asr25.md%2C%20their%20public%20summary%2Fchecks%2Fmanifest%20files%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2F%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_audit%2F%2C%20and%20accessible%20private%20geometry%2Fresponse%20packets.%20Inspect%20actual%20committed%2Fstaged%20code%3B%20ASR25%20may%20already%20have%20been%20committed%20since%20this%20prompt%20was%20written.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Verify%20the%20existing%20evidence%20and%20report%20its%20actual%20scope%3A%20mp%2Fduel10%20and%20mp%2Fduel7%20each%20have%20one%20accepted%20conditional%20mounted-point%20configuration.%20Do%20not%20claim%20there%20is%20no%20stock%20success%20or%20count%20the%20preserved%20duel10%20result%20twice.%0D%0A2.%20Assess%20spatial%2Fvisual%20sufficiency%20separately%20from%20numeric%20acceptance%3A%20one%20chart%20and%20one%20held-out%20block%20in%20duel10%3B%20a%20large%20residual%20and%20minimally%20sufficient%20grid%20corroboration%20in%20duel7%3B%20neither%20result%20is%20a%20complete%20room.%0D%0A3.%20Select%20at%20most%20two%20new%20original-stock%20ROI%20scopes%20with%20up%20to%20four%20independently%20inventoried%20visible%20fixtures%20each.%20At%20least%20one%20scope%20must%20test%20two%20or%20more%20distinct%20motivated%20fixtures%20and%20surrounding%20receiver%20surfaces%20beyond%20a%20repeat%20of%20one%20fitted%20chart%3B%20preflight%20their%20observable%20influence.%20Use%20geometry%20and%20visible%20evidence%20before%20opening%20new%20fit%2Ffinal%20values.%20Keep%20the%20published%20examples%20as%20development%2Fregression%20baselines%2C%20not%20fresh%20blind%20tests.%0D%0A4.%20Freeze%20an%20existing-model%20baseline%3A%20modern%20DiffuseIntegral%20plus%20explicitly%20conditional%20appearance%20transfer%2C%20fixed%20gauge%20and%20bounded%20nuisance.%20Finite-radius%20implementation%2C%20full%20fields%2C%20MD3%20and%20whole-map%20export%20are%20not%20prerequisites%20for%20this%20diagnostic%20proof.%0D%0A5.%20Choose%20geometric%20coverage%2C%20shadow%2Fdark%20controls%2C%20color%2Fbrightness%20motif%2C%20multi-source%20interaction%2C%20source-removal%20and%20independent%20visual%2Fspatial%20acceptance%20measures%20before%20fitting.%20Specify%20assistance%2C%20new%20fold%2Fscene%20independence%2C%20work%20limits%20and%20failed-case%20accounting.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-decision.md%20and%20a%20versioned%20viability%20protocol%20under%20tools%2Frend2%2Faesthetic_relighting%2Fpolicies%2F.%20Bound%20the%20next%20coding%20effort%20to%20one%20M%20integration%20plus%20at%20most%20one%20M%20repair%20before%20a%20mandatory%20decision.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20decision%20credits%20existing%20stock%20results%2C%20preserves%20their%20hashes%2Flimitations%2C%20and%20identifies%20a%20specific%20missing%20multi-fixture%20demonstration%20rather%20than%20restarting%20first-source%20discovery.%0D%0A-%20The%20frozen%20experiment%20has%20concrete%20original%20inputs%2C%20visible-fixture%20inventory%2C%20model%20assumptions%2C%20independent%20evaluation%2C%20artifact%20requirements%20and%20an%20exit%20budget.%0D%0A-%20A%20viable%20already%20implemented%20path%20is%20reused%3B%20if%20inputs%20or%20required%20semantics%20are%20missing%2C%20the%20result%20is%20an%20explicit%20Blocked%20or%20targeted%20decision%2C%20not%20speculative%20infrastructure%20work.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20rerun%20the%20entire%2057-map%20audit%2C%20require%20a%20complete%20new%20modern%20evaluator%20before%20testing%2C%20silently%20retune%20the%20ASR24%2F25%20thresholds%2C%20claim%20a%20remaster-quality%20room%20from%20one%20fitted%20chart%2C%20or%20invent%20a%20probability%20of%20whole-corpus%20completion.%0D%0ADeliver%20a%20concrete%20evidence-backed%20decision%20or%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20experiment%20plus%20its%20decision.%20You%20may%20rewrite%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%20and%20future%20prompts%20or%20add%20tasks%20within%20the%20data-only%20aesthetic%20objective.%20Log%20every%20change%2C%20preserve%20prior%20frozen%20failures%2C%20and%20give%20each%20new%20task%20a%20standalone%20English%20prompt%2C%20S%2FM%20size%20and%20a%20measurable%20exit%20condition.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-34%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Record%20approved%20scope%2Fpolicy%2Fprompt%20changes%20in%20Plan%20Change%20Log%2C%20with%20fresh%20independent%20final%20evidence%20when%20old%20final%20results%20informed%20the%20change.%0D%0A) ptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20the%20narrow%20end-to-end%20stock-region%20path%20required%20by%20the%20frozen%20viability%20protocol%3A%20visible%20fixed%20fixtures%2C%20native%20response%2C%20joint%20RGB%20fit%2C%20saved%20parameters%2C%20replay%20and%20a%20reviewable%20spatial%2Fvisual%20result.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-decision.md%2C%20the%20frozen%20viability%20policy%20under%20tools%2Frend2%2Faesthetic_relighting%2Fpolicies%2F%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2Frun.py%20and%20report.py%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_audit%2Fpacket.py%2C%20Aesthetic%3A%3AFitAndValidate%2FSelectSparse%2C%20native%20coverage%2Fsource%20support%2C%20and%20the%20ASR24%2F25%20accepted%20and%20failed%20witnesses.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Map%20each%20required%20operation%20to%20existing%20code%20and%20reuse%20it.%20If%20the%20path%20is%20already%20complete%2C%20verify%20it%20and%20record%20no%20implementation%20needed%20rather%20than%20creating%20another%20fitter%20or%20protocol.%0D%0A2.%20Add%20only%20the%20missing%20cohort%2Fadapter%20wiring%20to%20evaluate%20up%20to%20four%20independently%20supplied%20visible%20fixtures%20together%20on%20each%20selected%20original%20ROI%2C%20preserving%20source%20families%2C%20individual%20masks%2Fapproximations%20and%20fixed%20fixture-local%20poses.%0D%0A3.%20Use%20the%20same%20native%20modern%20evaluator%2C%20occupancy%20assumptions%20and%20appearance%2Fgauge%20in%20unit-response%20preflight%2C%20fitting%20and%20frozen%20replay.%20Distinguish%20blocked%2C%20unavailable%2C%20zero%20and%20positive%20response%3B%20keep%20dark%2Fshadow%20controls.%0D%0A4.%20Freeze%20original%20multi-surface%20receiver%20support%20and%20disjoint%20folds%20before%20target-dependent%20fitting.%20Jointly%20fit%20allowed%20RGB%20and%20the%20same%20bounded%20nuisance%20baseline%3B%20do%20not%20add%20free%20positions%20or%20backgrounds%20to%20force%20success.%0D%0A5.%20Save%20actual%20source%20parameters%2C%20geometry%2Fsupport%20references%2C%20policy%2Finput%20identities%2C%20conditional%20limitations%20and%20per-receiver%20predictions.%20Reload%20these%20data%20without%20the%20optimizer%20to%20reproduce%20the%20score%20and%20source-removal%20response.%0D%0A6.%20Reuse%20existing%20report%2FOBJ%2Fplate%20generation%20and%20add%20only%20the%20contextual%20views%20needed%20to%20assess%20combined%20light%20motifs%2C%20receiver%20normals%2C%20shadows%20and%20unexplained%20regions%20at%20fixed%20presentation%20settings.%0D%0A7.%20Run%20meaningful%20native%2Fstock-protocol%20regressions%20and%20the%20declared%20replay%20checks.%20Enforce%20the%20integration%20budget%3B%20one%20additional%20repair%20requires%20a%20written%20causal%20decision.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20selected%20real-stock%20ROI%20can%20execute%20the%20complete%20existing-model%20path%20and%20produce%20replayable%20data%20plus%20the%20frozen%20review%20packet%2C%20including%20honest%20failed%2Funavailable%20configurations.%0D%0A-%20All%20independently%20supplied%20fixtures%20and%20original%20receiver%20scopes%20remain%20auditable%3B%20known%20blockers%20are%20retained.%0D%0A-%20No%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20hand-written%20fitted%20power%20or%20synthetic%20scene%20is%20substituted%20for%20the%20substantive%20stock%20experiment.%0D%0A-%20The%20implementation%20is%20complete%20within%20its%20narrow%20scope%3B%20aesthetic%20acceptance%20is%20left%20to%20the%20separate%20frozen%20evaluation.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20implement%20finite%20near-field%20physics%2C%20a%20new%20renderer%2C%20a%20full-map%20scheduler%2C%20a%20new%20storage%20schema%20or%20a%20new%20optimizer%20merely%20to%20run%20this%20proof.%20Do%20not%20silently%20convert%20the%20exploratory%20point%2Fstand-off%20approximation%20into%20final%20production%20physics.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20the%20existing%20real%20path%20with%20meaningful%20verification.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20sketch%2C%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20stub%20or%20report%20for%20the%20required%20integration.%20Reuse%20already%20complete%20operations.%20If%20the%20scoped%20work%20exceeds%20M%20or%20its%20required%20decision%20is%20absent%2C%20document%20the%20concrete%20split%2Fblocker%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20work%20or%20decide%20a%20new%20product%20policy.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-35%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Do%20not%20silently%20change%20acceptance%20criteria%20or%20the%20plan%20direction%3B%20propose%20a%20specific%20decision%20task%20if%20a%20branch%20is%20needed.%0D%0A) [AR-36](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-36%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Decision%20%2F%20Experiment%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Decide%20Go%2C%20Repair%2C%20Pivot%20or%20Stop%20on%20a%20real%20stock%20region%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20the%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%2C%20concurrent%20and%20staged%20changes%2C%20including%20ASR25.%20Verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20actual%20lamps%2Fscreens%2FBSP%2Fmodel%20emission%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20an%20independently%20motivated%20probable%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20Historical%20type%2C%20XYZ%2C%20power%20and%20exactLight%20certification%20are%20not%20aesthetic%20eligibility%20requirements.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0APreserve%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20meanings.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%20and%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Record%20modern-model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADetermine%20whether%20the%20existing%20aesthetic%20reconstruction%20path%20produces%20a%20coherent%20multi-fixture%20original-stock%20result%2C%20and%20explicitly%20authorize%2C%20redirect%20or%20stop%20the%20next%20work%20instead%20of%20following%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0ARead%20the%20frozen%20viability%20protocol%2Fdecision%2C%20complete%20stock-region%20fitted%20data%20and%20replay%2Freview%20packets%2C%20original%20source%2Freceiver%20evidence%2C%20and%20ASR24%2F25%20reference%20results.%20Required%20policy%20and%20saved-data%20inputs%20must%20exist%3B%20do%20not%20infer%20them%20from%20this%20prompt%20or%20from%20another%20chat.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Verify%20that%20all%20hypotheses%2C%20geometry%2Fassistance%2C%20nuisance%20capacity%2C%20validation%20selection%20and%20evaluation%20limits%20froze%20before%20opening%20the%20new%20final%20data.%20Already%20exposed%20ASR24%2F25%20rows%20remain%20development%20evidence.%20A%20one-source%2Fsingle-chart%20repeat%20cannot%20satisfy%20the%20new%20multi-fixture%20gate%3B%20record%20it%20only%20as%20regression%20or%20partial%20evidence.%0D%0A2.%20Evaluate%20every%20declared%20ROI%20and%20its%20dark%2Fshadow%20controls%2C%20source%20attachments%2C%20color%20motifs%2C%20relative%20brightness%2C%20multi-source%20interactions%2C%20nuisance%2Fparameter%20bounds%20and%20unsupported%20area.%0D%0A3.%20Review%20spatial%2Fcontextual%20visual%20output%20at%20a%20fixed%20declared%20presentation%3B%20a%20geometric%20overlay%20or%20a%20successful%20interval-MSE%20percentage%20alone%20cannot%20establish%20convincing%20room%20lighting.%0D%0A4.%20Check%20the%20loaded%20saved%20data%20and%20frozen-background%20source-removal%20behavior.%20Do%20not%20refit%20after%20final%20scoring%20or%20claim%20unmeasured%20regions%20as%20reconstructed.%0D%0A5.%20Issue%20one%20explicit%20outcome%3A%20Go-Narrow%20for%20justified%20expansion%20to%20independent%20regions%3B%20Repair-Wiring%20for%20a%20specific%20bounded%20defect%3B%20Pivot-Appearance%2FBackground%20for%20model%20mismatch%3B%20Assisted-Geometry%20for%20a%20disclosed%20evidence%2Fannotation%20need%3B%20Blocked-Inputs%3B%20or%20Stop-Current-Approach.%0D%0A6.%20For%20every%20non-Go%20branch%2C%20name%20the%20causal%20evidence%2C%20the%20exact%20bounded%20research%2Fdecision%2Fimplementation%20deliverable%2C%20its%20size%20and%20a%20fresh%20exit%20test.%20Add%20standalone%20tasks%2Fprompts%20when%20needed%3B%20do%20not%20assign%20a%20generic%20%27improve%20the%20solver%27%20task.%0D%0A7.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20pilot-acceptance.md%20with%20numeric%2Fspatial%2Fvisual%20results%2C%20all%20failures%2C%20uncertainty%2C%20approved%20next%20scope%20and%20a%20plan%20revision.%20No%20automatic%20corpus%20rollout%20follows%20from%20this%20verdict.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20substantive%20stock%20result%20is%20accepted%20only%20if%20it%20meets%20the%20previously%20frozen%20multi-fixture%2C%20geometry%2C%20appearance%20and%20review%20gates%3B%20otherwise%20the%20failure%20is%20retained%20with%20a%20useful%20causal%20decision.%0D%0A-%20The%20verdict%20distinguishes%20existing%20one-source%20feasibility%2C%20new%20coherent-region%20viability%2C%20full%20modern-model%20adequacy%20and%20whole-map%20readiness.%0D%0A-%20Repairs%20stay%20within%20the%20fixed%20short%20budget%3B%20exceeding%20it%20requires%20a%20new%20explicit%20decision%20before%20more%20infrastructure%20work.%0D%0A-%20The%20roadmap%20and%20future%20prompts%20reflect%20the%20chosen%20branch%2C%20including%20a%20stop%20or%20scope%20reduction%20when%20evidence%20does%20not%20support%20continuation.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20treat%20this%20decision%20as%20a%20promised%20success%2C%20rewrite%20final%20criteria%20after%20seeing%20results%2C%20iterate%20on%20the%20same%20final%20patch%20indefinitely%2C%20classify%20every%20failure%20as%20missing%20engineering%2C%20or%20report%20conditional%20point-proxy%20fit%20as%20a%20complete%20relit%20map.%0D%0ADeliver%20a%20concrete%20evidence-backed%20decision%20or%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20experiment%20plus%20its%20decision.%20You%20may%20rewrite%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%20and%20future%20prompts%20or%20add%20tasks%20within%20the%20data-only%20aesthetic%20objective.%20Log%20every%20change%2C%20preserve%20prior%20frozen%20failures%2C%20and%20give%20each%20new%20task%20a%20standalone%20English%20prompt%2C%20S%2FM%20size%20and%20a%20measurable%20exit%20condition.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-36%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Record%20approved%20scope%2Fpolicy%2Fprompt%20changes%20in%20Plan%20Change%20Log%2C%20with%20fresh%20independent%20final%20evidence%20when%20old%20final%20results%20informed%20the%20change.%0D%0A) [AR-01](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-01%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Research%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20S%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Audit%20the%20current%20relighting%20path%20and%20reconcile%20work%20in%20progress%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20a%20current%2C%20evidence-backed%20implementation%20and%20capability%20map%20for%20data-only%20aesthetic%20relighting%2C%20including%20any%20stock_aesthetic%20work%20that%20has%20appeared%20since%20this%20plan%20was%20written.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20git%20status%2Flog%2C%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-fit-asr20.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-sparse-source-selection-asr21.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-iterative-discovery-asr22.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-blind-benchmark-asr23.md%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2F%2C%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Faesthetic_fit.%7Bh%2Ccpp%7D%2C%20aesthetic_sparse.inc%2C%20iterative_discovery.%7Bh%2Ccpp%7D%2C%20shared%2Frd-rend2%2Flightrecon%2F%7Bemitter_shape%2Cshape_selection%2Cemitter_support%2Cproducer%2Cfield_builder%2Ccontributions%7D.%2A%2C%20and%20the%20existing%20fixture%20catalogues.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20docs%2Frend2-stock-aesthetic-asr24.md%20and%20docs%2Frend2-stock-discovery-asr25.md%20now%20document%20substantive%20one-source%20stock%20results%20and%20a%20full%20inventory%20audit.%20Reuse%20their%20frozen%20summaries%2Fchecks%20and%20current%20code%2C%20including%20staged%20changes%2C%20instead%20of%20repeating%20a%20zero-result%20baseline%20audit.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Inventory%20completed%20code%2C%20experimental%20adapters%2C%20uncommitted%20work%2C%20frozen%20measurements%20and%20genuinely%20missing%20connections.%20Identify%20one%20authoritative%20implementation%20for%20fitting%2C%20source%20support%2C%20visibility%20and%20each%20observation%20domain.%0D%0A2.%20Trace%20a%20real%20fixture%20from%20shader%2Fimage%20geometry%20through%20source%20support%2C%20receiver%20selection%2C%20modern%20coefficients%2C%20fitting%2C%20validation%20and%20saved%20data.%20Distinguish%20available%20catalogue%20masks%20from%20masks%20actually%20consumed%20by%20native%20evaluation.%0D%0A3.%20Document%20finite%20effective-radius%20rejection%20in%20the%20current%20Shape%20validator%2C%20rangeWorld%27s%20existing%20culling%20semantics%2C%20footprint%2Fsupport%20assumptions%2C%20and%20the%20distinction%20between%20aesthetic%20eligibility%20and%20exactLight.%0D%0A4.%20Record%20present%20stock%20results%20and%20their%20scopes%2C%20original%20asset%2Fcapture%20availability%20and%20the%20actual%20repository%20remote.%20Mark%20concurrent%20uncommitted%20work%20as%20provisional%20rather%20than%20duplicating%20or%20certifying%20it.%0D%0A5.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fimplementation-audit.md%20with%20a%20reuse%20map%2C%20blocker%20table%2C%20authoritative%20artifact%20paths%20and%20recommended%20minimal%20pilot%20route.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20audit%20names%20the%20current%20revision%2C%20local%20modifications%2C%20APIs%2C%20native%20callers%20and%20specific%20blockers%20with%20file%20references.%0D%0A-%20Completed%20ASR09%2F11%20mask%20extraction%20and%20ASR20%E2%80%9323%20numerical%20tools%20are%20distinguished%20from%20missing%20native%2Forchestration%20integration.%0D%0A-%20Claims%20about%20stock%20success%2C%20scene-wide%20coverage%20and%20tests%20are%20tied%20to%20actual%20receipts%3B%20unknowns%20remain%20explicit.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20implement%20a%20second%20fitter%2C%20start%20a%20new%20benchmark%2C%20modify%20concurrent%20work%2C%20or%20treat%20an%20old%20report%27s%20failure%20as%20proof%20that%20the%20current%20code%20still%20fails.%20This%20task%20produces%20a%20substantive%20research%20document%2C%20not%20a%20speculative%20rewrite.%0D%0AProduce%20a%20substantive%20written%20investigation%20with%20code%2Fdata%20references%2C%20limitations%20and%20actionable%20conclusions.%20Read%20relevant%20primary%20sources%20if%20external%20technical%20evidence%20is%20needed.%20Separate%20measured%20facts%2C%20assumptions%20and%20recommendations.%20Do%20not%20start%20speculative%20implementation%20or%20new%20experiments%20outside%20the%20stated%20investigation.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-01%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20research%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) le%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AAdopt%20a%20measurable%20product%20contract%20for%20aesthetic%20relighting%20that%20can%20accept%20useful%20modern%20reconstructions%20without%20historical%20source%20certification%2C%20while%20preventing%20fictitious%20success%20and%20unmotivated%20lights.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20current%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fimplementation-audit.md%20if%20present%2C%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-source-baseline-asr01.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-blind-benchmark-asr23.md%2C%20and%20the%20current%20stock_aesthetic%20policies%2Fresults.%20Evaluate%20the%20real%20repository%20state%20rather%20than%20assuming%20any%20report%20is%20final.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20two%20bounded%20one-source%20stock%20configurations%20are%20accepted%20under%20the%20ASR24%2F25%20conditional%20policy%2C%20but%20neither%20map%20is%20fully%20relit.%20Distinguish%20the%20early%20viability%20protocol%20from%20final%20whole-map%20acceptance%3B%20original-grid%20corroboration%20is%20not%20a%20mandatory%20universal%20requirement%20for%20every%20visually%20motivated%20A%20fixture.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Define%20A%20as%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%20with%20compatible%20lighting%2Fgeometry%2C%20B%20as%20a%20plausible%20unobserved%20source%20with%20independent%20spatial%2Fdirectional%2Fshadow%20support%2C%20C%20as%20supported%20lighting%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20as%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%0D%0A2.%20Separate%20aesthetic-use%20eligibility%20from%20exactLight%2C%20historical%20type%2FXYZ%2Fintensity%2C%20statistical%20existence%20confidence%20and%20calibrated%20SI.%20Define%20allowed%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20explicit%20assistance%20levels.%0D%0A3.%20Freeze%20region%2Fmap%20acceptance%20measures%3A%20geometric%20safety%2C%20motif%2Fcolor%20consistency%2C%20relative%20brightness%20ordering%2C%20shadows%2Fdark%20controls%2C%20parsimony%2C%20worst-region%20errors%2C%20independent%20visual%20review%20and%20material%2Ftransfer%20sensitivity.%20Choose%20and%20justify%20numerical%20thresholds%20before%20final%20evaluation.%0D%0A4.%20Define%20coverage%20denominators%20from%20the%20original%20map%20inventory%20and%20fixed%20eligible%20surface%2Fregion%20census.%20Track%20A%2FB%20direct%20reconstruction%2C%20C%20field%20support%20and%20D%2Foriginal%20fallback%20separately%3B%20processing%20a%20map%20or%20preserving%20its%20original%20lighting%20does%20not%20count%20as%20reconstructing%20it.%0D%0A5.%20Set%20distinct%20gates%20for%20a%20pilot%20region%2C%20one%20whole%20map%2C%20at%20least%20half%20the%20frozen%20map%20corpus%2C%20and%20the%20full%20corpus.%20Define%20allowed%20D%20budgets%20and%20unresolved%20high-salience%20regions%3B%20no%20substantial%20D%20area%20may%20be%20hidden%20in%20a%20%27complete%27%20label.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20a%20versioned%20policy%20and%20decision%20rationale%20under%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2F%20and%20tools%2Frend2%2Faesthetic_relighting%2Fpolicies%2F.%20Exercise%20the%20policy%20on%20successful%2C%20empty%2C%20unknown%2C%20area-vs-point%20and%20false-lamp%20counterexamples.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20machine-readable%2C%20versioned%20acceptance%20policy%20and%20readable%20decision%20record%20exist%2C%20including%20hard%20gates%20and%20tunable%20aesthetic%20measures.%0D%0A-%20A%2FB%20can%20be%20accepted%20for%20remaster%20use%20with%20unknown%20historical%20identity%3B%20C%2FD%20have%20meaningful%20outputs%20and%20explicit%20coverage%20accounting.%0D%0A-%20Historical%20XYZ%20matching%20and%20exact%20luxel%20equality%20are%20not%20stock%20acceptance%20requirements%3B%20low%20aggregate%20RGB%20error%20alone%20cannot%20pass.%0D%0A-%20Any%20newly%20required%20implementation%20work%20is%20added%20to%20the%20plan%20as%20bounded%2C%20independently%20executable%20prompts.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20inherit%20historical-identifiability%20gates%20as%20aesthetic%20blockers%2C%20lower%20thresholds%20after%20inspecting%20final-test%20failures%2C%20or%20invent%20physical%20calibration.%20Do%20not%20turn%20a%20workflow%20decision%20into%20runtime%20integration.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-02%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) 0Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADetermine%20how%20original%20encoded%20lightmaps%20can%20guide%20modern%20relighting%20without%20being%20silently%20treated%20as%20linear%20irradiance%2C%20and%20recommend%20a%20bounded%2C%20implementable%20comparison%20model.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0ARead%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Faesthetic_fit.%7Bh%2Ccpp%7D%2C%20shared%2Frd-rend2%2Flightrecon%2F%7Bdecode%2Cbake_forward%2Cemitter_shape%2Camplitude_fit%7D.%2A%2C%20docs%2Frend2-local-light-evidence-asr08.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-fit-asr20.md%2C%20and%20current%20source%20texture%2Fmaterial%20capture%20and%20calibration%20contracts.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Trace%20original%20RGB8%20decoding%2C%20quantization%2C%20saturation%2C%20shared-peak%20compression%2C%20material%20variation%20and%20the%20existing%20forward-encoded%20appearance%20route.%0D%0A2.%20Compare%20a%20fixed%20forward%20appearance%20operator%2C%20explicitly%20approximate%20target%20transforms%2C%20same-material%20relative%2Fcontrast%20features%2C%20and%20any%20independently%20supported%20linear%20observations.%20State%20which%20quantities%20each%20can%20and%20cannot%20support.%0D%0A3.%20Specify%20a%20fixed%20brightness%2Fcolor%20gauge%20and%20how%20exposure%2C%20receiver%20response%2C%20RGB%20power%20and%20background%20avoid%20becoming%20mutually%20compensating%20free%20parameters.%0D%0A4.%20Define%20training-only%20treatment%20of%20any%20fitted%20material-group%20nuisance%2C%20its%20bounds%20and%20validation%20behavior.%20Explain%20why%20current%20PBR%20albedo%20or%20an%20assumed%20inverse%20gamma%20cannot%20recover%20historical%20incident%20light%20by%20itself.%0D%0A5.%20Recommend%20the%20minimum%20pilot%20model%20plus%20a%20later%20multi-material%20extension.%20Identify%20source-color%20priors%2C%20reflection%20tint%2C%20dark%20controls%20and%20saturation%20handling%20that%20preserve%20the%20scene%27s%20visual%20motifs.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fappearance-domain-research.md%20with%20equations%2C%20explicit%20assumptions%2C%20adversarial%20cases%20and%20concrete%20API%20changes.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20report%20distinguishes%20encoded%20appearance%2C%20approximate%20linear%20appearance%20and%20physically%20attested%20irradiance.%0D%0A-%20The%20proposed%20loss%20has%20a%20consistent%20domain%2C%20fixed%20gauge%2C%20nonnegative%20source%20parameters%20and%20bounded%20nuisance%20behavior.%0D%0A-%20The%20recommendation%20can%20be%20implemented%20with%20existing%20evaluators%20and%20identifies%20experiments%20that%20would%20falsify%20it.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20claim%20to%20recover%20Raven%27s%20bake%20recipe%2C%20divide%20encoded%20lightmaps%20by%20modern%20albedo%2C%20subtract%20encoded%20RGB%20as%20energy%2C%20or%20write%20code%20that%20merely%20renames%20RGB8%20as%20linear.%20Do%20not%20require%20historical%20calibration%20to%20permit%20a%20clearly%20labeled%20aesthetic%20approximation.%0D%0AProduce%20a%20substantive%20written%20investigation%20with%20code%2Fdata%20references%2C%20limitations%20and%20actionable%20conclusions.%20Read%20relevant%20primary%20sources%20if%20external%20technical%20evidence%20is%20needed.%20Separate%20measured%20facts%2C%20assumptions%20and%20recommendations.%20Do%20not%20start%20speculative%20implementation%20or%20new%20experiments%20outside%20the%20stated%20investigation.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-03%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20research%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-04](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-04%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Decision%20%2F%20Experiment%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Freeze%20the%20modern%20lighting%20and%20appearance%20policy%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AChoose%20one%20explicit%20modern%20source-transport%20and%20appearance%20policy%20for%20the%20relighting%20output%2C%20including%20the%20meaning%20of%20source%20size%20and%20artistic%20range%2C%20so%20subsequent%20fitting%20has%20a%20fixed%20model.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fappearance-domain-research.md%2C%20current%20acceptance%20policies%2C%20shared%2Frd-rend2%2Flightrecon%2Femitter_shape.%2A%2C%20source-support%20code%2C%20tr_lightrecon_types.h%2C%20and%20small%20owned%20emitter%20fixtures.%20If%20a%20required%20policy%20is%20missing%2C%20record%20the%20missing%20artifact%20instead%20of%20guessing%20its%20semantics.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20the%20existing%20modern%20DiffuseIntegral%20plus%20conditional%20forward%20encoding%20already%20supports%20useful%20stock%20fits.%20Use%20the%20real-stock%20viability%20decision%20to%20justify%20which%20model%20gaps%20need%20new%20implementation%3B%20do%20not%20require%20a%20full%20evaluator%20rewrite%20merely%20to%20replay%20or%20extend%20the%20working%20baseline.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Choose%20a%20physically%20motivated%20inverse-square%20far-field%20point%2Fspot%20model%20with%20finite%20near-source%20behavior%2C%20an%20explicit%20effective-size%20interpretation%2C%20smooth%20artistic%20range%20behavior%20and%20a%20normalized%20angular%20convention.%0D%0A2.%20Choose%20rectangle%2C%20original%20clipped-sheet%20and%20tube%20integration%20semantics%2C%20one%2Ftwo-sided%20emission%2C%20treatment%20of%20tube%20end%20caps%2C%20and%20justified%20segmented%20approximations.%0D%0A3.%20Distinguish%20effective%20radiometric%20size%2C%20occupied%20fixture%20geometry%2C%20contact%20clearance%20and%20range%20metadata.%20Do%20not%20silently%20change%20rangeWorld%20from%20culling%20metadata%20into%20a%20different%20canonical%20semantic.%0D%0A4.%20Run%20small%20near%2Ffar%2C%20energy-normalization%2C%20scale%2Fgauge%2C%20grazing-angle%20and%20mounting%20experiments.%20Choose%20one%20model%20and%20record%20rejected%20alternatives%20and%20known%20approximations.%0D%0A5.%20Choose%20the%20appearance-domain%20route%2C%20material%20assumptions%2C%20fixed%20transfer%2Fgauge%20and%20baseline%2Fbackground%20limits.%20Set%20tolerances%20for%20native%20evaluation%2C%20numerical%20refinement%20and%20approximation%20sensitivity.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20a%20versioned%20modern-lighting%20policy%2C%20appearance%20policy%20and%20implementation%20decision%20record.%20Update%20downstream%20prompt%20details%20through%20artifact%20contracts%2C%20without%20making%20them%20depend%20on%20this%20conversation.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Equations%2C%20units%2C%20limits%2C%20source-size%2Foccupancy%20distinctions%2C%20angular%20normalization%20and%20range%20behavior%20are%20unambiguous%20and%20frozen.%0D%0A-%20Aesthetic%20model%20identity%20can%20be%20hashed%20and%20saved%20alongside%20existing%20canonical%20data%20without%20reinterpreting%20old%20assets.%0D%0A-%20The%20chosen%20policy%20permits%20area%2Fspot%20improvements%20even%20when%20an%20old%20point-light%20bake%20has%20lower%20literal%20RGB%20error.%0D%0A-%20Required%20implementation%20changes%20are%20split%20into%20S%2FM%20tasks%3B%20unresolved%20model%20choices%20are%20not%20delegated%20as%20vague%20coding%20instructions.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20search%20for%20a%20historical%20falloff%2C%20fit%20exposure%20jointly%20with%20source%20power%20without%20fixing%20the%20gauge%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%2C%20or%20choose%20a%20convenient%20near-field%20clamp%20without%20stating%20its%20physical%2Fapproximation%20meaning.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-04%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) ction.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Integrate%20the%20selected%20model%20into%20a%20native%20modern-only%20evaluator%20or%20narrowly%20scoped%20modern%20branch%2C%20reusing%20prepared%20geometry%2C%20visibility%2C%20occupancy%20and%20parameter%20structures.%0D%0A2.%20Implement%20finite%20near-field%20response%2C%20inverse-square%20far-field%20behavior%2C%20normalized%20spot%20cones%2C%20artistic%20range%20transitions%20and%20exact%20parameter-domain%20validation.%0D%0A3.%20Keep%20effective%20source%20size%20distinct%20from%20occupied%20fixture%20volume.%20Use%20the%20selected%20mounting%2Femission-node%20policy%3B%20an%20arbitrary%20empty-air%20sphere%20is%20not%20a%20mounting%20proof.%0D%0A4.%20Include%20all%20model%2Fradius%2Frange%2Fangular%20parameters%20in%20prepared%20identities%20and%20diagnostics.%20Preserve%20unavailable%20results%20for%20unknown%20optical%20paths.%0D%0A5.%20Add%20independent%20numerical%20references%20and%20tests%20for%20near%2Ffar%20limits%2C%20continuity%2C%20radius%2Frange%20changes%2C%20cone%20edges%2C%20blocked%20and%20unknown%20paths%2C%20and%20unchanged%20historical%20outputs.%0D%0A6.%20Expose%20the%20full%20evaluated%20behavior%20through%20the%20existing%20aesthetic%20fitting%20path%20and%20offline%20output%20metadata%3B%20a%20disconnected%20helper%20is%20insufficient.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Nonzero%20effective-size%20point%20and%20spot%20sources%20are%20evaluable%2C%20fit-ready%20and%20reproducible%20under%20the%20frozen%20modern%20policy.%0D%0A-%20Numerical%20references%20validate%20the%20chosen%20equations%20and%20documented%20approximation%20error%3B%20no%20nonfinite%20or%20negative%20response%20is%20admitted.%0D%0A-%20Historical%20source%20expansion%2C%20exact-contact%20guards%2C%20storage%20semantics%20and%20regression%20witnesses%20retain%20their%20previous%20behavior.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20remove%20validation%20checks%20globally%2C%20implement%20only%20a%20preview%20formula%20or%20a%20renderer-specific%20approximation%2C%20or%20claim%20that%20a%20softened%20ideal%20source%20proves%20a%20finite%20bulb%20body%20exists.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-05%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision. ncoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AMake%20all%20supported%20finite%20modern%20sources%20obey%20the%20same%20frozen%20energy%2C%20visibility%2C%20range%20and%20appearance%20conventions%20as%20the%20point%2Fspot%20evaluator.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20modern-lighting%20policy%2C%20Shape%3A%3APrepare%2FEmissionNodes%2C%20SourceSupport%2C%20existing%20rectangle%2Foriginal-union%2Ftube%20quadrature%20tests%20and%20the%20aesthetic%20fitting%20API.%20Reuse%20implemented%20finite%20integration%20rather%20than%20replacing%20it%20with%20another%20solver.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Implement%20or%20reconcile%20finite%20rectangle%2C%20original%20clipped-sheet%20and%20tube%20response%20under%20the%20chosen%20policy%2C%20including%20angular%20and%20range%20conventions.%0D%0A2.%20Keep%20emission%20area%2C%20radiance-like%20amplitude%20and%20point-intensity-like%20amplitude%20explicit.%20Preserve%20one%2Ftwo-sided%20semantics%20and%20the%20declared%20open-cylinder%20end-cap%20rule.%0D%0A3.%20Implement%20the%20allowed%20segmented-tube%20approximation%20with%20saved%20component%20membership%2C%20bounded%20geometric%2Fradiometric%20error%20and%20common%20physical-fixture%20identity.%0D%0A4.%20Preserve%20holes%2C%20gaps%20and%20separate%20components%20in%20irregular%20emission.%20Unsupported%20geometry%20must%20remain%20Unknown%20or%20have%20a%20separately%20measured%20bounded%20approximation.%0D%0A5.%20Verify%20partially%20blocked%20sources%2C%20receiver%20footprints%20and%20quadrature%20convergence%20against%20independent%20higher-accuracy%20references.%0D%0A6.%20Integrate%20all%20finite-source%20parameters%2C%20approximation%20choices%20and%20source%20support%20into%20fitting%20and%20prepared%2Fcache%20identities.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Rectangle%2C%20exact%20sheet%20and%20tube%20hypotheses%20pass%20native%20integration%20and%20cross-type%20normalization%2Freference%20tests.%0D%0A-%20Changing%20segmentation%20or%20quadrature%20does%20not%20silently%20change%20total%20emission%2C%20fixture%20count%20or%20canonical%20identity.%0D%0A-%20Area%20and%20tube%20behavior%20is%20used%20by%20actual%20fitting%20and%20exported%20diagnostics%2C%20not%20just%20standalone%20tests.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20approximate%20every%20panel%20with%20a%20point%2C%20fill%20a%20nonrectangular%20mesh%27s%20bounding%20rectangle%2C%20invent%20a%20tube%20body%20from%20aspect%20ratio%2C%20or%20change%20historical%20polygon%20expansion.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-06%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) 20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AUse%20the%20existing%20shader%2Fimage-clipped%20BSP%20emitting%20polygons%20as%20actual%20native%20aesthetic%20source%20support%20instead%20of%20substituting%20a%20whole%20BSP%20face.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Frend2%2Fbsp_fixtures%2Fcatalogue.py%20and%20its%20geometry%2Fmask%20helpers%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fshape_selection%2F%2C%20shared%2Frd-rend2%2Flightrecon%2F%7Bshape_selection%2Cemitter_support%2Cemitter_shape%7D.%2A%2C%20and%20current%20stock_aesthetic%20adapters.%20The%20catalogue%20already%20clips%20RGB%20mask%20support%3B%20the%20native%20bridge%20is%20the%20gap%20to%20verify.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20ASR24%2F25%20use%20catalogue-derived%20fixture%20evidence%20but%20accept%20fixed%20mounted-point%20proxies%2C%20not%20exact%20native%20clipped%20area%20evaluation.%20Preserve%20this%20useful%20regression%20path%20while%20proving%20the%20missing%20finite-footprint%20integration.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Read%20the%20existing%20catalogue%27s%20pinned%20BSP%2C%20shader%2C%20image%2C%20UV-transform%2C%20stage%2C%20triangle-alias%2C%20component%20and%20uncertainty%20records.%0D%0A2.%20Build%20an%20immutable%20native%20footprint%20lease%20with%20explicit%20interpretation%20and%20generation%20identity.%20Prove%20clipped%20polygons%20lie%20within%20their%20pinned%20source%20triangles%20and%20declared%20support%20bounds.%0D%0A3.%20Preserve%20lit%2Fdark%20gaps%2C%20multiple%20stages%2C%20component%20identities%20and%20per-layer%20color%20provenance.%20Retain%20optical%20alpha%2Flayer%20uncertainty%20separately%20from%20conditional%20RGB-mask%20support.%0D%0A4.%20Extend%20only%20the%20necessary%20modern-only%20geometry%2Fsupport%20adapter.%20Newly%20clipped%20vertices%20must%20not%20be%20mislabeled%20as%20unchanged%20original%20BSP%20triangle%20vertices.%0D%0A5.%20Feed%20the%20actual%20footprint%20to%20native%20placement%20and%20quadrature%2C%20association%2C%20fitting%20and%20diagnostics.%20Whole-face%20approximation%20remains%20an%20explicit%20alternative%20with%20measured%20bounds.%0D%0A6.%20Verify%20repeated%2Fclamped%2Faffine%20UVs%2C%20thin%20strips%2C%20dark%20gaps%2C%20overlapping%20UVs%2C%20irregular%20unions%2C%20stale%20image%20hashes%20and%20independent%20blockers.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20stock%20catalogue%20fixture%20can%20be%20evaluated%20and%20fitted%20using%20its%20clipped%20emitting%20geometry%20with%20original%20surface%20identity%20and%20image%20provenance.%0D%0A-%20Area%2C%20centroid%20and%20source%20contribution%20match%20independent%20polygon%2Fquadrature%20references%20within%20the%20declared%20tolerance.%0D%0A-%20Unsupported%20optics%20stay%20conditional%2Funknown%3B%20old%20.jkobs%20data%20and%20historical%20source-support%20interpretation%20remain%20readable%20and%20unchanged.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20rebuild%20already%20working%20mask%20extraction%2C%20promote%20RGB%20thresholds%20to%20optical%20transparency%2C%20pretend%20a%20fitted%20rectangle%20is%20the%20exact%20luminous%20mask%2C%20or%20stop%20at%20JSON%20import%20without%20native%20fitting%20integration.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-07%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-08](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-08%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Connect%20static%20model%20luminous%20regions%20without%20inventing%20occlusion%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AMake%20eligible%20original%20static-model%20luminous%20fixtures%20usable%20as%20aesthetic%20source%20anchors%20with%20correct%20instance%20transforms%2C%20emitting%20support%20and%20explicit%20occlusion-participation%20limits.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Frend2%2Fmd3_instances%2F%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fmodel_emission%2F%2C%20docs%2Frend2-md3-instances-asr10.md%2C%20docs%2Frend2-model-emission-asr11.md%2C%20native%20fixture-footprint%20adapters%2C%20SourceSupport%20and%20static%20visibility%20interfaces.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20ASR25%20identifies%20model-source%20discovery%20as%20a%20specific%20Hoth%20gap.%20Entity%20model-reference%20counts%20are%20not%20lamp%20counts%3B%20implement%20the%20existing%20instance%2Femission%20bridge%20only%20for%20locally%20supportable%20selected%20cases.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Reuse%20existing%20MD3%20decoding%2C%20skin%2Fmaterial%20resolution%2C%20selected%20poses%2C%20instance%20manifests%20and%20clipped%20emission%20polygons.%0D%0A2.%20Adapt%20eligible%20instance-local%20regions%20into%20pinned%20world-space%20source%20support%20with%20correct%20nonuniform-scale%20area%2Fnormals%20and%20stable%20instance%2Fcomponent%20identities.%0D%0A3.%20Define%20explicit%20geometry-provider%20identities%20for%20model%20facets%3B%20do%20not%20fabricate%20BSP%20ordinals%20or%20collapse%20repeated%20instances%20into%20one%20source.%0D%0A4.%20Handle%20emitting%20self-facets%20with%20narrowly%20scoped%20identity%20rules%20while%20preserving%20opaque%20housing%20and%20all%20independent%20walls.%20Missing%20or%20unverified%20model%20occupancy%20remains%20explicit.%0D%0A5.%20Route%20admitted%20sources%20through%20modern%20evaluation%2C%20association%20and%20fitting%3B%20retain%20unsupported%20dynamic%2Fanimated%2Funknown-state%20records%20as%20coverage%20gaps.%0D%0A6.%20Verify%20transformed%20instances%2C%20repeated%20assets%2C%20mixed%20skins%2C%20BSP-duplicate%20suppression%2C%20housing%20occlusion%2C%20unknown%20alpha%20and%20missing%20models.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20one%20eligible%20stock%20static-model%20fixture%20reaches%20native%20geometry%2Fevaluation%20and%20data-only%20fitting%20or%20receives%20a%20precise%20diagnosed%20blocker.%0D%0A-%20Instance%20identity%2C%20transformed%20area%20and%20occlusion%20provenance%20survive%20output%20and%20replay.%0D%0A-%20Unsupported%20model%20geometry%20never%20becomes%20empty%20space%20or%20a%20silently%20transparent%20light%20housing.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20import%20every%20model%20as%20an%20opaque%20box%2C%20invent%20bake-time%20mover%20states%2C%20add%20runtime%20model%20lighting%2C%20or%20claim%20catalogued%20emission%20automatically%20participates%20in%20the%20old%20bake.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-08%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A)
