lack.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AProvide%20admissible%20modern%20source%20poses%2C%20size%20bounds%20and%20local%20stand-off%20alternatives%20tied%20to%20real%20fixtures%2C%20without%20reviving%20global%20room-wide%20position%20search.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20SourceSupport%2C%20regions_support.cpp%2C%20source_orientation.h%2C%20modern%20emitter%20policies%2C%20exact%20fixture%20adapters%20and%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2F.%20The%20current%20stock%20experiment%27s%20fixed%20outward%20point%20offset%20is%20an%20explicit%20approximation%20to%20review%2C%20not%20a%20universal%20fixture%20dimension.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Derive%20initial%20pose%20and%20bounded%20alternatives%20from%20actual%20luminous%20geometry%2C%20housing%20dimensions%2C%20mounting%20side%20and%20explicitly%20recorded%20assumptions.%0D%0A2.%20Distinguish%20point%2Fspot%20proxy%20size%2C%20finite%20occupied%20body%2C%20emitting%20sheet%2C%20recess%20and%20front%20clearance.%20Allow%20a%20nonzero%20stand-off%20only%20with%20fixture%20evidence%20or%20a%20clearly%20bounded%20approved%20approximation.%0D%0A3.%20Validate%20every%20alternative%20against%20retained%20brushes%20and%20independent%20visual%20geometry%3B%20exclude%20only%20precisely%20identified%20emitting%20self-geometry%20where%20justified.%0D%0A4.%20Expose%20bounded%20size%2C%20direction%2C%20cone%20and%20local%20pose%20ranges%20to%20fitting%20with%20stable%20fixture-family%20IDs.%20No%20alternative%20may%20jump%20to%20another%20fixture%20or%20pass%20through%20a%20wall.%0D%0A5.%20Preserve%20multiple%20plausible%20poses%20as%20alternatives%20and%20retain%20Unknown%20when%20support%20is%20insufficient.%0D%0A6.%20Test%20wall%2Fceiling%20mounts%2C%20recesses%2C%20thin%20sheets%2C%20tube%20tangent%20bodies%2C%20crossing%20walls%2C%20wrong-side%20emission%2C%20unknown%20alpha%20and%20disconnected%20fixtures.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Point%2Fspot%2Farea%2Ftube%20hypotheses%20have%20auditable%20source-to-fixture%20pose%20bounds%20and%20correct%20emitting-side%20admission.%0D%0A-%20The%20modern%20fitter%20can%20use%20safe%20local%20alternatives%20while%20unrelated%20solid%2Foptical%20constraints%20remain%20enforced.%0D%0A-%20Fixed%20arbitrary%20offsets%20are%20replaced%20by%20explicit%20supported%20policies%20or%20remain%20labeled%20bounded%20approximations.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20clamp%20negative%20distances%20into%20validity%2C%20exempt%20a%20whole%20mounting%20brush%2C%20treat%20all%20unknown%20geometry%20as%20free%2C%20or%20move%20lamps%20anywhere%20in%20the%20room%20to%20reduce%20RGB%20error.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-09%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) ncoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AReduce%20avoidable%20Unknown%20source%2Freceiver%20paths%20using%20original%20local%20geometry%20and%20bounded%20refinement%20while%20preserving%20genuinely%20unavailable%20geometry%20and%20optics.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20visibility%20extraction%2C%20BSP%20support%20predicates%2C%20original%20patch%20control%20geometry%2C%20receiver_support%2C%20ASR17%2F18%20diagnostics%20and%20current%20fixture%20influence%20reports.%20Work%20only%20on%20diagnosed%20local%20failure%20classes.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20use%20the%20fixed%20ASR25%20failure%20witnesses.%20A%20whole-map%20static-map%20guard%20is%20a%20separate%20controller%20limitation%20from%20an%20actual%20local%20Unknown%20support%3B%20do%20not%20conflate%20them%20or%20repeat%20corpus-wide%20diagnostics.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Classify%20Unknowns%20as%20patch%20approximation%2C%20bounds-only%20omission%2C%20numerical%20boundary%20ambiguity%2C%20missing%20instance%20state%2C%20unknown%20optics%20or%20stale%20identity.%0D%0A2.%20Implement%20bounded%20local%20patch%20refinement%20or%20conservative%20separation%20proofs%20for%20the%20selected%20resolvable%20classes%2C%20preserving%20the%20original%20error%20envelope%20until%20a%20proof%2Frefinement%20replaces%20it.%0D%0A3.%20Keep%20refined%20geometry%20and%20tolerance%20in%20the%20scene%2Fprepared%20identity.%20Apply%20the%20same%20interpretation%20to%20source%20admission%2C%20visibility%20and%20receiver%20comparison.%0D%0A4.%20Report%20unresolved%20optical%2Fmover%2Fmissing-data%20cases%20with%20original%20support%20IDs%20and%20scoped%20coverage%20loss.%0D%0A5.%20Compare%20exact%20failure%20reasons%20before%2Fafter%20on%20fixed%20owned%20and%20stock%20witnesses%2C%20including%20known%20collisions%20and%20alpha%20controls.%0D%0A6.%20Expose%20work%2Fmemory%20limits%20and%20stop%20explicitly%20when%20the%20required%20refinement%20cannot%20be%20completed.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20diagnosed%20avoidable%20local%20Unknown%20class%20is%20resolved%20using%20retained%20evidence%20with%20quantified%20error%2Fwork%2C%20or%20its%20external%20blocker%20is%20precisely%20demonstrated.%0D%0A-%20All%20known%20solid%20intersections%2C%20unknown%20optics%20and%20unavailable%20model%20states%20retain%20safe%20behavior.%0D%0A-%20Coverage%20reports%20show%20original%20and%20resolved%20denominators%3B%20no%20removed%20failure%20disappears%20from%20accounting.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20globally%20lower%20patch%20accuracy%2C%20convert%20conservative%20AABBs%20to%20empty%20geometry%2C%20change%20historical%20tracing%20tolerances%20indiscriminately%2C%20or%20make%20Unknown%20mean%20black.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-10%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-11](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-11%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Commission%20source-to-receiver%20influence%20and%20observable%20sampling%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AEnsure%20real%20fixture%20hypotheses%20are%20compared%20against%20geometrically%20relevant%20original%20surfaces%20and%20explicit%20dark%2Fshadow%20controls%2C%20with%20complete%20diagnoses%20for%20zero%20or%20unavailable%20response.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Faesthetic_region.h%2C%20lightrecon_aesthetic_tool.cpp%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2F%2C%20ASR14%2F15%20patch%2Fassociation%20code%2C%20native%20Shape%20prepared%20coefficients%20and%20original%20receiver%20footprints.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20ASR24%20already%20implemented%20native%20outward%20receiver-hit%20selection%2C%20zero-dummy-RGB%20unit-response%20coverage%2C%20disjoint%20folds%20and%20a%20frozen%20source-removal%20audit.%20Reuse%20them%20and%20diagnose%20only%20remaining%20multi-fixture%2Fmulti-surface%20sampling%20gaps%3B%20the%20old%20mp%2Fduel4%20zero-response%20study%20is%20preserved%20historical%20evidence%2C%20not%20a%20reason%20to%20recreate%20commissioning%20from%20scratch.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Compute%20unit-amplitude%20source-response%20columns%20before%20reading%20target%20RGB%2C%20using%20the%20exact%20evaluator%2C%20occupancy%20policy%20and%20source%20variants%20that%20fitting%20will%20use.%0D%0A2.%20Separate%20emitting-side%2C%20receiver-facing%2C%20distance%2Frange%2C%20blocked%2C%20unknown-path%2C%20zero-coefficient%20and%20positive-coefficient%20reasons%20per%20original%20receiver.%0D%0A3.%20Choose%20training%2Fvalidation%2Ffinal%20partitions%20from%20original%20geometry%2C%20material%20groups%2C%20spatial%20blocks%20and%20footprint%20gaps.%20Retain%20known-dark%2Fblocked%20controls%20and%20account%20for%20every%20excluded%20receiver.%0D%0A4.%20Avoid%20choosing%20a%20material%20solely%20because%20it%20has%20many%20samples%20while%20every%20candidate%20has%20zero%20influence%20there.%20Require%20a%20declared%20minimum%20informative%20support%20without%20hiding%20unsupported%20parts%20of%20the%20declared%20region.%0D%0A5.%20Keep%20every%20alternative%20on%20comparable%20evaluation%20denominators%20or%20explicitly%20report%20the%20common%20supported%20scope%20and%20its%20coverage%20loss.%0D%0A6.%20Produce%20a%20diagnostic%20matrix%2Fplate%20for%20the%20old%20mp%2Fduel4%20zero-response%20fixture%20and%20additional%20visible%20fixtures%2C%20with%20original%20IDs%20and%20causal%20failure%20counts.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Zero-response%20and%20unknown-response%20cases%20are%20mechanically%20distinguishable%20and%20reproducible.%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20one%20real%20stock%20fixture%20has%20independently%20selected%20informative%20multi-block%20receiver%20support%2C%20or%20a%20precise%20blocker%20explains%20why%20no%20tested%20fixture%20does.%0D%0A-%20The%20sampling%20path%20never%20uses%20final%20RGB%20to%20choose%20membership%20and%20includes%20contradiction%2Fdark%20controls.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20fix%20the%20zero-response%20problem%20by%20assigning%20nonzero%20coefficients%2C%20making%20unknown%20rays%20transparent%2C%20dropping%20shadowed%20samples%2C%20or%20selecting%20only%20receivers%20that%20already%20fit.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-11%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) ions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AChoose%20a%20small%20but%20substantive%20original-stock%20pilot%20with%20visible%2C%20geometrically%20motivated%20sources%20and%20freeze%20its%20evidence%2C%20assistance%20level%2C%20alternatives%20and%20acceptance%20policy%20before%20fitting.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20current%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2F%20results%2C%20exact%20fixture%20catalogues%2C%20influence%2Freceiver%20diagnostics%2C%20current%20acceptance%20and%20modern-lighting%20policies%2C%20and%20original%20stock%20captures%2Fassets.%20Reuse%20an%20existing%20valid%20pilot%20instead%20of%20starting%20over.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20a%20minimal%20viability%20protocol%20and%20its%20go%2Frepair%2Fpivot%20decision%20must%20already%20exist%20as%20named%20artifacts%20under%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2F%20or%20tools%2Frend2%2Faesthetic_relighting%2Fpolicies%2F.%20This%20task%20expands%20to%20a%20representative%20pilot%20bank%20under%20the%20chosen%20final%20modern%20policy%3B%20it%20must%20not%20postpone%20the%20first%20stock%20demonstration%20or%20rerun%20ASR24%2F25%20as%20newly%20blind%20evidence.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Select%20one%20simple%20interior%20region%20with%20several%20real%20visible%20fixtures%2C%20useful%20color%2Fbrightness%20motifs%2C%20static%20occluders%20and%20independent%20receiver%20blocks.%20Include%20difficult%2Fnegative%20companion%20regions%20selected%20before%20scoring.%0D%0A2.%20Freeze%20all%20selected%20regions%2C%20fixture%20identities%2C%20masks%2Fapproximations%2C%20geometric%20alternatives%2C%20materials%2C%20transfers%2C%20budgets%20and%20train%2Fvalidation%2Ffinal%20partitions%20with%20hashes.%0D%0A3.%20Declare%20any%20manual%20fixture%20inventory%2C%20mask%20correction%2C%20material%20grouping%20or%20local%20pose%20prior.%20Separate%20assistance%20levels%3B%20supplied%20anchors%20cannot%20be%20reported%20as%20blind%20discovery.%0D%0A4.%20Check%20that%20proposed%20A%20sources%20are%20motivated%20by%20visible%20fixtures%20and%20illumination%20structure%3B%20do%20not%20demand%20historically%20exact%20RGB%2Ftype%2Fcoordinates.%0D%0A5.%20Choose%20a%20limited%20background%20model%20and%20compare%20alternatives%20that%20could%20explain%20the%20same%20observation%2C%20including%20C%2FD%20where%20appropriate.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fpilot-decision.md%20and%20a%20machine-readable%20pilot%20manifest.%20Update%20the%20plan%20based%20on%20concrete%20feasibility%20evidence.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20pilot%20bank%20uses%20original%20stock%20data%2C%20multiple%20source%20hypotheses%20and%20meaningful%20independent%20positive%2Fnegative%20evaluation%20support.%0D%0A-%20All%20assumptions%2C%20manual%20interventions%20and%20selection%20decisions%20are%20frozen%20before%20final%20scoring.%0D%0A-%20A%20geometry%2Finput%20failure%20yields%20a%20documented%20decision%20and%20bounded%20repair%20tasks%2C%20not%20substitution%20of%20a%20synthetic%20scene%20labeled%20as%20stock.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20keep%20trying%20regions%20until%20one%20produces%20a%20favorable%20final%20score%2C%20use%20held-out%20RGB%20to%20choose%20fixtures%2Fmaterials%2C%20or%20demand%20an%20untouched%20full-map%20autonomous%20solver%20before%20obtaining%20a%20declared%20assisted%20pilot.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-12%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AFit%20complete%20modern%20fixed-fixture%20RGB%20amplitudes%20under%20the%20chosen%20appearance%20policy%2C%20with%20balanced%20observations%20and%20bounded%20background%2C%20using%20the%20existing%20aesthetic%20fitter.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20pilot%20and%20appearance%20policies%2C%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Faesthetic_fit.%7Bh%2Ccpp%7D%2C%20aesthetic_sparse.inc%2C%20native%20modern%20prepared%20responses%20and%20the%20original%20receiver%20snapshots.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20ASR24%2F25%20demonstrate%20that%20the%20existing%20fixed-fixture%20aesthetic%20RGB%20fit%20works%20on%20real%20observations.%20Preserve%20those%20native%20numerical%20reports%20as%20regressions.%20Implement%20only%20policy%2Fevaluator%2Fmaterial%20integration%20gaps%20justified%20by%20the%20stock%20viability%20decision%2C%20not%20a%20replacement%20fitter.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Route%20the%20authoritative%20modern%20coefficients%20and%20actual%20footprint%20geometry%20into%20Aesthetic%3A%3AFitAndValidate%3B%20remove%20adapter%2Fmodel%20mismatches%20rather%20than%20cloning%20another%20fitter.%0D%0A2.%20Implement%20the%20selected%20comparison%20operator%2C%20robust%20interval%2Fappearance%20loss%2C%20saturation%20behavior%20and%20material-group%20handling%20in%20its%20declared%20domain.%0D%0A3.%20Fit%20nonnegative%20RGB%20amplitudes%20with%20finite%20bounds%20and%20a%20fixed%20gauge.%20Fit%20only%20the%20allowed%20training%20background%2Fnuisance%20with%20the%20same%20capacity%20in%20the%20no-local%20baseline.%0D%0A4.%20Balance%20spatial%20regions%2C%20normal%20groups%2C%20material%20groups%20and%20represented%20area.%20Preserve%20correlated%20sample%20identities%20and%20comparable%20alternative%20coverage.%0D%0A5.%20Record%20contributions%2C%20zero-response%20diagnostics%2C%20bound%20hits%2C%20parameter%20correlations%2C%20held-out%20readiness%20and%20approximation%20sensitivity.%0D%0A6.%20Validate%20relative-linear%20references%2C%20encoded%20appearance%2C%20outliers%2C%20duplicate%20sampling%2C%20correlated%20lamps%2C%20zero%20channels%20and%20stock%20pilot%20inputs.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20real%20pilot%20can%20run%20from%20frozen%20input%20to%20fitted%20fixed-geometry%20source%20parameters%20and%20validation%20selection%20with%20no%20hand-written%20fitted%20powers.%0D%0A-%20Background%20cannot%20absorb%20arbitrary%20per-receiver%20error%20and%20low%20RGB%20residual%20cannot%20overrule%20geometric%2Fevidence%20hard%20gates.%0D%0A-%20The%20implementation%20is%20integrated%20with%20native%20evaluation%20and%20saves%20the%20complete%20replayable%20fitted%20configuration.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20fit%20stock%20RGB8%20directly%20as%20physical%20irradiance%2C%20refit%20the%20exposure%20gauge%20with%20power%2C%20give%20every%20luxel%20its%20own%20free%20background%2C%20or%20stop%20after%20implementing%20an%20isolated%20NNLS%20example.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-13%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) d%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AExtend%20the%20fixed-fixture%20aesthetic%20fit%20to%20supported%20radius%2Fsize%2C%20spot%20axis%2Fcone%20and%20small%20fixture-local%20pose%20alternatives%20without%20changing%20physical%20fixture%20identity%20or%20using%20global%20position%20search.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20frozen%20fixture-local%20pose%20bounds%2C%20modern-lighting%2Fappearance%20policies%2C%20Aesthetic%3A%3AFixture%20variants%2C%20source-support%20checks%2C%20Shape%20quadrature%20and%20the%20pilot%27s%20fixed-geometry%20baseline.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Implement%20bounded%20parameterization%20or%20deterministic%20variants%20for%20the%20dimensions%20allowed%20by%20actual%20fixture%20evidence%3B%20unsupported%20dimensions%20remain%20frozen.%0D%0A2.%20Jointly%20refit%20RGB%20on%20training%20for%20every%20accepted%20geometry%20variant%2C%20normalize%20energy%20correctly%20when%20area%2Fsize%20changes%2C%20and%20invalidate%20prepared%20coefficients%20on%20every%20relevant%20parameter%20change.%0D%0A3.%20Validate%20each%20complete%20source%20support%20and%20independent%20optical%20path%3B%20for%20continuous%20moves%20certify%20the%20required%20support%2Fsweep%20or%20evaluate%20explicitly%20separate%20static%20hypotheses.%0D%0A4.%20Freeze%20geometry%20choices%20before%20validation%20selection%20and%20all%20choices%20before%20final%20audit.%20Keep%20equivalent%20variants%20and%20meaningful%20parameter%20uncertainty.%0D%0A5.%20Compare%20against%20the%20fixed-geometry%20baseline%20to%20show%20whether%20each%20extra%20degree%20of%20freedom%20improves%20held-out%20visual%20structure%20without%20moving%20away%20from%20the%20fixture.%0D%0A6.%20Test%20radius%2Frange%20coupling%2C%20narrow%20cones%2C%20wall%20mounts%2C%20body%20size%2C%20source%20aliases%20and%20repeated%20deterministic%20runs.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20All%20enabled%20parameter%20changes%20have%20explicit%20fixture-derived%20bounds%2C%20support%20admission%20and%20replayable%20identities.%0D%0A-%20An%20improved%20fit%20remains%20attached%20to%20its%20real%20fixture%20and%20does%20not%20exchange%20increased%20brightness%20for%20arbitrary%20size%2Fpose%20changes%20without%20diagnostics.%0D%0A-%20Unsupported%20or%20weakly%20observed%20parameters%20stay%20fixed%2Fambiguous%20rather%20than%20receiving%20invented%20precision.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20move%20sources%20through%20walls%2C%20expand%20a%20panel%20beyond%20its%20luminous%20footprint%2C%20optimize%20positions%20across%20the%20room%2C%20use%20final%20targets%20for%20parameter%20search%2C%20or%20label%20local%20coordinate%20convergence%20as%20a%20global%20optimum.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-14%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) %20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AMake%20aesthetic%20relighting%20reviewable%20through%20reproducible%20data-only%20visual%20diagnostics%20and%20spatial%20reports%2C%20without%20installing%20a%20runtime%20lighting%20consumer.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2Freport.py%2C%20current%20geometry%20plates%2FOBJ%20exports%2C%20Fields%2FAttribution%20diagnostics%2C%20original%20receiver%20geometry%2C%20source-response%20data%20and%20frozen%20appearance%2Facceptance%20policies.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20reuse%20stock_aesthetic%2Freport.py%2C%20stock_audit%2Fpacket.py%2C%20geometry%20plates%2C%20hypothesis%20files%20and%20frozen%20response%20plates.%20Their%20existing%20geometric%2Fsingle-chart%20evidence%20is%20not%20a%20whole-room%20relighting%20preview%3B%20add%20only%20the%20spatial%2Fcontextual%20review%20needed%20by%20the%20selected%20decision.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Generate%20original-reference%20and%20reconstructed-appearance%20plates%20using%20one%20fixed%20declared%20display%20transform%2C%20exposure%20and%20color%20convention%3B%20show%20source%20footprints%2C%20normals%2C%20shadows%20and%20original%20receiver%20support.%0D%0A2.%20Provide%20geometry-aware%20views%20or%20offline%20surface-atlas%2Fmesh%20previews%20sufficient%20to%20assess%20visible%20light%20motifs%2C%20wall%20sides%2C%20occlusion%2C%20dark%20gaps%20and%20room%20transitions.%0D%0A3.%20Show%20direct%20A%2FB%20contributions%2C%20C%2Fbackground%20and%20D%2Foriginal%20fallback%20as%20distinct%20layers%2C%20plus%20missing%2Funknown%20geometry%20masks.%0D%0A4.%20Report%20per-region%2Fmaterial%2Fnormal-group%20and%20worst-block%20errors%2C%20color%2Fbrightness%20ordering%2C%20bound%20hits%2C%20source-to-fixture%20distance%20and%20source-count%2Falternative%20information.%0D%0A5.%20Include%20all%20selected%20pilot%20regions%2C%20including%20failures.%20Record%20assistance%2C%20approximations%20and%20the%20named%20review%20method%3B%20automated%20score%20output%20is%20not%20a%20fabricated%20human%20approval.%0D%0A6.%20Make%20all%20report%20images%2C%20sampled%20data%20and%20summary%20metrics%20reproducible%20from%20frozen%20artifacts%2C%20with%20licensed%20geometry%2Fimages%20kept%20in%20the%20appropriate%20private%20output.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20reviewer%20can%20inspect%20the%20actual%20pilot%20result%20and%20its%20failure%20areas%20without%20reading%20solver%20internals%20or%20launching%20a%20modified%20game.%0D%0A-%20Original%20and%20reconstructed%20views%20use%20comparable%20fixed%20presentation%3B%20auto%20exposure%20or%20selective%20views%20cannot%20hide%20overlighting.%0D%0A-%20Coverage%2Funknown%2Ffallback%20and%20source%20provenance%20are%20visible%20alongside%20quality%20metrics.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20modify%20world%20shading%2C%20add%20lights%20to%20maps%2C%20build%20a%20GPU%20viewer%2Fwebsite%20as%20a%20substitute%20for%20reconstruction%2C%20or%20deliver%20only%20an%20attractive%20screenshot%20with%20no%20spatial%2Ferror%20provenance.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-15%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20mus oded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ASpecify%20how%20global%20sun%2Fsky%2C%20source-linked%20bounce%20and%20unexplained%20lighting%20distributions%20should%20participate%20in%20the%20modern%20aesthetic%20reconstruction%20without%20being%20converted%20into%20fictitious%20lamps.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20shared%2Frd-rend2%2Flightrecon%2F%7Bsun_fit%2Cindirect%2Ctransport_mesh%2Cfield_builder%2Cfield_basis%2Cprobes%2Ccontributions%7D.%2A%2C%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Fiterative_discovery.%2A%2C%20docs%2Frend2-local-light-evidence-asr08.md%2C%20field%2Fappearance%20reports%20and%20the%20ASR23%20false-local-source%20cases.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Map%20existing%20physical%2C%20relative%20and%20observation-appearance%20field%20capabilities%2C%20geometry%2Fvisibility%20assumptions%2C%20material%20requirements%20and%20remaining%20implementation%20gaps.%0D%0A2.%20Compare%20bounded%20source-linked%20bounce%20with%20conservative%20C%20appearance%20fields%20and%20global%20sun%2Fsky%20hypotheses.%20Describe%20a%20useful%20pilot-to-whole-map%20progression.%0D%0A3.%20Define%20attribution%2Fidentifiability%20limits%20and%20how%20omitted%20transport%2C%20sky%20portals%2C%20colored%20reflections%20and%20movers%20affect%20confidence%20and%20coverage.%0D%0A4.%20Specify%20spatial%20support%20that%20respects%20walls%2Fopenings%20and%20prevents%20a%20free%20background%20from%20explaining%20arbitrary%20direct-light%20structure.%0D%0A5.%20Recommend%20practical%20model%20families%2C%20independent%20evidence%20requirements%2C%20numerical%20controls%20and%20alternatives%20for%20missing%20albedo%2Ftransfer.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fnonlocal-lighting-research.md%20with%20concrete%20APIs%2C%20data%20contracts%20and%20a%20bounded%20experiment%20matrix.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20report%20identifies%20which%20existing%20routes%20can%20represent%20C%20and%20which%20quantities%20remain%20unavailable.%0D%0A-%20Sun%2C%20local%20direct%20light%2C%20bounce%2C%20appearance%20background%20and%20D%20fallback%20have%20distinct%20semantics%20and%20attribution.%0D%0A-%20Recommendations%20permit%20useful%20aesthetic%20approximations%20without%20claiming%20full%20GI%20or%20requiring%20impossible%20historical%20knowledge.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20treat%20a%20doorway%20or%20reflected%20hotspot%20as%20a%20lamp%20by%20default%2C%20subtract%20RGB8%20components%20as%20energy%2C%20infer%20angular%20sky%20radiance%20from%20mean%20RGB%2C%20or%20propose%20unlimited%20per-receiver%20residual%20fields.%0D%0AProduce%20a%20substantive%20written%20investigation%20with%20code%2Fdata%20references%2C%20limitations%20and%20actionable%20conclusions.%20Read%20relevant%20primary%20sources%20if%20external%20technical%20evidence%20is%20needed.%20Separate%20measured%20facts%2C%20assumptions%20and%20recommendations.%20Do%20not%20start%20speculative%20implementation%20or%20new%20experiments%20outside%20the%20stated%20investigation.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-17%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20research%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) ded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AFreeze%20a%20useful%20but%20restrained%20global%2Fbackground%20model%20that%20reduces%20false%20lamps%20and%20preserves%20the%20visual%20composition%20of%20actual%20stock%20scenes.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fnonlocal-lighting-research.md%2C%20pilot%20diagnostics%2C%20ASR23%20sun%2Fsky%20and%20bounce%20controls%2C%20modern%2Fappearance%20policies%20and%20the%20available%20native%20global%2Ffield%20evaluators.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Compare%20no-local%20baselines%20with%20identical%20nuisance%20capacity%2C%20explicit%20sun%2Fsky%20candidates%2C%20source-linked%20transport%20where%20supported%20and%20bounded%20C%20distributions.%0D%0A2.%20Use%20controlled%20false-lamp%20cases%20and%20a%20preselected%20stock%20interior%2Fexterior%20bank%20with%20training%2C%20validation%20and%20independent%20final%20partitions.%0D%0A3.%20Choose%20background%20dimensionality%2C%20smoothness%2C%20wall-aware%20support%2C%20material%20assumptions%2C%20energy%2Fpower%20bounds%20and%20uncertainty%2Fattribution%20reporting.%0D%0A4.%20Measure%20whether%20model%20capacity%20suppresses%20real%20visible%20lamps%20or%20explains%20away%20high-frequency%20direct-light%20structure%3B%20include%20source-removal%20and%20dark-control%20checks.%0D%0A5.%20Select%20the%20simplest%20supported%20model%20that%20meets%20the%20declared%20aesthetic%20policy.%20Retain%20alternatives%20when%20source-vs-field%20attribution%20is%20not%20distinguishable.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20versioned%20nonlocal%2Fattribution%20policies%20and%20a%20decision%20record.%20Split%20any%20unresolved%20implementation%20expansion%20into%20concrete%20S%2FM%20tasks.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20chosen%20global%2FC%20model%20has%20explicit%20bounded%20capacity%20and%20a%20frozen%20validation%2Facceptance%20policy.%0D%0A-%20Neither%20a%20more%20flexible%20field%20nor%20an%20extra%20local%20lamp%20wins%20automatically%20from%20lower%20training%20error.%0D%0A-%20Modern%20aesthetic%20assumptions%20are%20stated%20independently%20of%20historical%20compiler%20identity%20and%20updated%20in%20the%20work%20plan.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20fit%20an%20unconstrained%20residual%20image%2C%20relabel%20background%20as%20calibrated%20GI%2C%20require%20perfect%20global%20decomposition%20before%20any%20local%20success%2C%20or%20tune%20a%20new%20background%20using%20final-test%20values.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-18%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) 0not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AInclude%20supported%20modern%20sun%2Fsky%20alternatives%20in%20the%20same%20aesthetic%20prediction%20and%20validation%20path%20as%20local%20sources%2C%20with%20correct%20geometric%20sky%20reach%20and%20no%20double%20attribution.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20nonlocal%20and%20modern-lighting%20policies%2C%20Solar%2Fsun_fit%2C%20original%20shader%2Fsky%20evidence%2C%20static%20visibility%2C%20environment%2Ffield%20representations%20and%20Aesthetic%2FJoint%20adapters.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Generate%20bounded%20directional%2Fenvironment%20alternatives%20from%20captured%20sky%2Fsource%20evidence%20and%20the%20chosen%20modern%20model%2C%20preserving%20uncertain%20direction%2Fcolor%2Fintensity%20where%20necessary.%0D%0A2.%20Evaluate%20actual%20sky%20visibility%20and%20occlusion%20for%20the%20same%20receiver%20support%20used%20by%20local%20fitting%3B%20unknown%20sky%20reach%20remains%20unavailable.%0D%0A3.%20Jointly%20fit%20allowed%20global%20and%20local%20amplitudes%20in%20a%20fixed%20gauge%2C%20sum%20compatible%20contributions%20before%20appearance%20encoding%2C%20and%20preserve%20one%20explicit%20ownership%20of%20sun%2Fsky%20terms.%0D%0A4.%20Compare%20solar-only%2C%20local-only%2C%20combined%20and%20C%20alternatives%20with%20equal%20nuisance%20capacity%20and%20frozen%20validation.%0D%0A5.%20Save%20the%20selected%20environment%2Fglobal%20configuration%20and%20equivalent%20alternatives%20with%20source%2Fprofile%20provenance.%0D%0A6.%20Verify%20direct%20sun%2C%20diffuse%20sky%2C%20indoor%20lamps%2C%20mixed%20sun%2Flocal%2C%20occluded%20sky%20and%20the%20false-local-source%20negative%20cases.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Supported%20stock%20exterior%2Finterior%20regions%20can%20include%20global%20illumination%20hypotheses%20without%20fabricated%20local%20lamps%20or%20duplicated%20sun%20contribution.%0D%0A-%20Known%20blocked%20and%20unknown%20sky%20paths%20remain%20distinct%3B%20no%20mean-grid%20direction%20becomes%20a%20certified%20sun%20automatically.%0D%0A-%20Global%20and%20local%20output%20parameters%20are%20replayable%20under%20the%20same%20aesthetic%20policy%20and%20data-only%20contracts.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20infer%20an%20exact%20Raven%20sun%2C%20remove%20uncertain%20sky%20blockers%2C%20assume%20every%20bright%20opening%20is%20a%20physical%20source%2C%20or%20implement%20CSM%2Ffog%2Frenderer%20consumers.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-19%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) oded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AImplement%20the%20selected%20bounded%20C%20representation%20and%20supported%20source-linked%20transport%20without%20inventing%20local%20emitters%20for%20unexplained%20illumination.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20nonlocal%2Fattribution%20policy%2C%20existing%20Fields%3A%3ABuild%2FObservationAppearance%2C%20source-linked%20Indirect%20evaluation%2C%20probe%20topology%2C%20visibility%20support%20and%20canonical%20conditional-field%20quantity%2Fcalibration%20contracts.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Reuse%20the%20chosen%20existing%20field%2Ftransport%20route%20and%20implement%20only%20missing%20native%20fitting%2Fintegration%20needed%20by%20the%20frozen%20policy.%0D%0A2.%20Represent%20supported%20nonlocal%20appearance%20or%20relative%20illumination%20with%20explicit%20quantities%2C%20domains%2C%20spatial%20support%2C%20bounds%20and%20provenance%3B%20leave%20unsupported%20angular%2FSI%20values%20unavailable.%0D%0A3.%20Constrain%20interpolation%20across%20walls%2Fopenings%20using%20retained%20geometry%20and%20preserve%20unresolved%20visibility%20instead%20of%20smoothing%20light%20through%20obstacles.%0D%0A4.%20Keep%20local%20direct%2C%20sun%2Fsky%2C%20source-linked%20bounce%2C%20C%20and%20D%20ownership%20explicit%2C%20with%20no%20automatic%20positive%20residual%20used%20to%20close%20an%20energy%20equation.%0D%0A5.%20Fit%20or%20build%20C%20from%20training%20only%20and%20evaluate%20its%20frozen%20capacity%20on%20validation%2Ffinal%20data%3B%20use%20the%20same%20competing%20capacity%20in%20local-light%20alternatives.%0D%0A6.%20Test%20reflected%20color%2C%20bright%20doorways%2C%20sparse%20observations%2C%20overflexible%20backgrounds%2C%20wall%20separation%20and%20missing%20material%2Ftransport%20evidence.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Ambiguous%20illumination%20can%20be%20saved%20as%20a%20useful%2C%20bounded%20C%20result%20with%20independent%20validation%20instead%20of%20an%20invented%20lamp.%0D%0A-%20C%20cannot%20reproduce%20arbitrary%20per-luxel%20targets%20or%20silently%20absorb%20every%20visible%20source.%0D%0A-%20Attribution%2C%20spatial%20scope%20and%20uncertainty%20survive%20native%20evaluation%20and%20output.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20turn%20raw%20RGB8%20residuals%20into%20additive%20irradiance%2C%20manufacture%20angular%20information%2C%20claim%20higher-bounce%20recovery%20from%20one-bounce%20support%2C%20or%20activate%20replacement%20of%20baked%20lighting%20in%20the%20game.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-20%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-21](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-21%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Unify%20evidence-aware%20sparse%20source%20selection%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ASelect%20a%20compact%2C%20visually%20motivated%20set%20of%20A%2FB%20sources%20under%20the%20authoritative%20aesthetic%20objective%20while%20preserving%20area%2Fspot%20hypotheses%20and%20attribution%20alternatives.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20Aesthetic%3A%3ASelectSparse%20and%20aesthetic_sparse.inc%2C%20ASR15%20association%2C%20native%20fixture%20adapters%2C%20modern%2Fglobal%2FC%20policies%20and%20ASR23%20duplicates%20plus%20finite-source%20rejection%20cases.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Use%20stable%20physical-fixture%2Fcomponent%2Ffamily%20identities%20and%20independent%20visible%20evidence%3B%20retain%20exact%20aliases%20without%20merging%20distinct%20neighboring%20lamps%20by%20color%20alone.%0D%0A2.%20Apply%20hard%20geometry%2Fdomain%20gates%20first%2C%20then%20evidence-aware%20validation%20and%20complexity%20costs%20that%20do%20not%20systematically%20replace%20visible%20panels%20with%20unsupported%20point%20proxies.%0D%0A3.%20Refit%20competing%20subsets%20and%20removals%20on%20training%2C%20compare%20frozen%20validation%20configurations%2C%20and%20retain%20equivalent%20alternatives%20instead%20of%20averaging%20them.%0D%0A4.%20Include%20the%20same%20global%2FC%20capacity%20in%20every%20competing%20configuration.%20A%20visible%20but%20unlit%20or%20unsupported%20fixture%20need%20not%20receive%20nonzero%20power.%0D%0A5.%20Diagnose%20duplicate%20emitters%2C%20correlated%20columns%2C%20weak%20observable%20contribution%2C%20bound%20hits%2C%20hidden-source%20evidence%20and%20source-versus-field%20ambiguity.%0D%0A6.%20Reproduce%20two-color%2C%20overlapping-white%2C%20area%2Fspot%2C%20redundant%20ceiling%20proxy%2C%20genuine%20multiple-fixture%20and%20reflected-light%20negative%20cases.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20selected%20source%20set%20has%20explicit%20fixture%20evidence%2C%20stable%20IDs%2C%20removal%20rationale%20and%20replayable%20alternatives.%0D%0A-%20Finite-source%20selection%20succeeds%20when%20the%20visible%20emitting%20geometry%20and%20independent%20appearance%20evidence%20support%20it.%0D%0A-%20Training-only%20improvement%2C%20pure%20color%20proximity%20or%20count%20penalties%20cannot%20silently%20decide%20aesthetic%20eligibility.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20optimize%20source%20count%20for%20future%20GPU%20slots%2C%20force%20every%20catalogue%20entry%20to%20emit%2C%20merge%20repeated%20instances%20into%20one%20lamp%2C%20or%20solve%20duplicates%20by%20suppressing%20all%20hidden%20sources.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-21%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) ck.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADiscover%20and%20validate%20remaining%20plausible%20sources%20after%20fitting%20visible%20fixtures%20and%20nonlocal%20illumination%2C%20while%20leaving%20unsupported%20residuals%20as%20C%2FD.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Flightrecon_test%2Fiterative_discovery.%2A%2C%20Hidden%2Fadaptive%2Fgrid%20proposal%20modules%2C%20frozen%20attribution%2Fevidence%20policies%2C%20the%20authoritative%20modern%20evaluator%20and%20sparse%20selection%20results.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Reuse%20the%20existing%20bounded%20discover%2Frefit%20loop%20and%20connect%20it%20to%20the%20same%20modern%2Fglobal%2FC%20prediction%20used%20for%20A%20sources.%0D%0A2.%20Require%20independent%20local%20spatial%2Fdirectional%2Fshadow%2Ffixture-context%20evidence%20for%20B%20search%3B%20a%20bright%20residual%20or%20a%20lower%20RGB%20error%20alone%20is%20insufficient.%0D%0A3.%20Generate%20bounded%20source%20families%20within%20original%20geometry%2C%20preserve%20alternatives%2C%20and%20reject%20known-solid%20or%20unknown%20required%20source%2Freceiver%20support.%0D%0A4.%20Jointly%20refit%20existing%20and%20proposed%20sources%20from%20training%2C%20pay%20the%20declared%20complexity%20cost%2C%20and%20require%20frozen%20validation%20improvement%20over%20the%20incumbent%20without%20unacceptable%20region%20regression.%0D%0A5.%20Recompute%20the%20unexplained%20structure%20after%20each%20accepted%20addition%20and%20stop%20explicitly%20on%20no%20supported%20improvement%2C%20ambiguity%2C%20limits%20or%20unavailable%20evidence.%0D%0A6.%20Verify%20that%20colored%20bounce%2C%20solar%20ambiguity%2C%20saturation%20and%20reflected%20doorways%20do%20not%20automatically%20become%20local%20lamps%3B%20retain%20all%20stop%2Faccept%2Freject%20receipts.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Additional%20B%20sources%20can%20be%20accepted%20for%20aesthetic%20use%20with%20provenance%20and%20uncertainty%20while%20historical%20identity%20stays%20unknown.%0D%0A-%20Unsupported%20residuals%20produce%20C%2FD%20decisions%20rather%20than%20forced%20local%20lights.%0D%0A-%20The%20final%20held-out%20partition%20cannot%20initialize%2C%20refit%2C%20accept%20or%20rerank%20sources.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20infer%20a%20source%20merely%20because%20the%20direct%20model%20is%20incomplete%2C%20copy%20the%20old%20historical%20residual%20objective%20into%20the%20modern%20path%2C%20scan%20unrestricted%20room%20XYZ%2C%20or%20call%20conditional%20B%20candidates%20historically%20confirmed.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-22%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A)
