ck.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADecide%20whether%20the%20assembled%20modern%20source%2Ffield%20pipeline%20is%20ready%20for%20whole-map%20stock%20reconstruction%20using%20blind%20controlled%20tests%20and%20independent%20visual%20stock-region%20review.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20aesthetic%20acceptance%20policy%2C%20current%20integrated%20native%20pipeline%2C%20docs%2Frend2-blind-benchmark-asr23.md%20and%20its%20untouched%20receipts%2C%20and%20predeclared%20stock%20pilot%2Fcompanion%20regions.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Preserve%20ASR23%20as%20historical%20evidence%20and%20create%20a%20new%20policy%2Fversion%20for%20the%20modern%20aesthetic%20objective%20rather%20than%20silently%20reinterpreting%20its%20source-recovery%20gates.%0D%0A2.%20Freeze%20development%2C%20validation%20and%20final%20scene%20families%20covering%20spots%2C%20finite%20panels%2Ftubes%2C%20multiple%20nearby%20lights%2C%20sunlight%2C%20colored%20bounce%2C%20openings%2C%20walls%2C%20masks%20and%20no-local-light%20cases.%0D%0A3.%20Evaluate%20detection%2Fgeometry%2C%20appearance%20composition%2C%20shadows%2C%20false%20unmotivated%20sources%20and%20C%2FD%20coverage%20separately.%20Historical%20type%2FXYZ%20truth%20is%20only%20a%20controlled%20diagnostic%2C%20not%20a%20stock%20requirement.%0D%0A4.%20Run%20relevant%20ablations%20with%20equal%20declared%20limits%20and%20the%20same%20authoritative%20fitter%3B%20verify%20area%2Fspot%20behavior%20contributes%20where%20appropriate%20rather%20than%20remaining%20a%20no-op.%0D%0A5.%20Audit%20leakage%2C%20comparable%20receiver%20denominators%2C%20assistance%2C%20model%20capacity%2C%20repeats%20and%20source%2Finput%20hashes.%0D%0A6.%20Issue%20a%20readiness%20decision.%20Turn%20failed%20causal%20classes%20into%20specific%20bounded%20tasks%20and%20update%20the%20roadmap%3B%20do%20not%20merely%20append%20more%20benchmarks%20indefinitely.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Independent%20evidence%20supports%20or%20rejects%20whole-map%20readiness%20under%20predeclared%20aesthetic%20gates.%0D%0A-%20Known%20duplicates%2C%20finite-source%20omissions%20and%20global-light%20ghosts%20have%20measured%20dispositions%2C%20including%20explicit%20unresolved%20limitations.%0D%0A-%20Any%20plan%20change%20is%20justified%20by%20evidence%20and%20preserves%20failed%2Ffrozen%20results.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20tune%20on%20final%20scenes%2C%20require%20exact%20historical%20source%20counts%20for%20stock%2C%20declare%20success%20from%20mean%20error%20while%20salient%20regions%20fail%2C%20or%20weaken%20geometry%20and%20Unknown%20handling%20to%20pass.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-23%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) ns%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0APrioritize%20the%20concrete%20input%2Ftool%20limitations%20measured%20by%20the%20original%2057-map%20audit%20and%20authorize%20only%20repairs%20that%20unlock%20a%20specified%20reconstruction%20scope.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-stock-discovery-asr25.md%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_audit%2Fresults%2Fv1%2F%7Bmaps.json%2Csummary.json%2Cchecks.json%2Cprotocol.json%7D%2C%20current%20actual%20files%2Fnative%20errors%2C%20the%20viability%20decision%20and%20the%20selected%20next%20map%20bank.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Recheck%20current%20file%20availability%20and%20measured%20causes%3A%2020%20missing%20captures%2C%2023%20whole-map%20dynamic%20vetoes%2C%2011%20catalogue%20work-budget%20maps%2C%20one%20polygon-budget%20map%20and%20two%20native%20capture-section%20refusals.%20Counts%20overlap%3B%20do%20not%20sum%20them%20as%20independent%20failed%20maps.%0D%0A2.%20Separate%20missing%20inputs%2C%20controller%20restrictions%20and%20bounded%20work%20limits%20from%20failed%20lighting-model%20evidence.%20Preserve%20per-map%20nulls%20and%20rejected%20fits.%0D%0A3.%20Choose%20the%20smallest%20repair%20branch%20needed%20by%20the%20approved%20next%20region%2Fmap%20bank%3A%20restore%20original%20evidence%2C%20complete%20bounded%20catalogue%20processing%2C%20or%20support%20locally%20justified%20dynamic-map%20scopes.%0D%0A4.%20Specify%20target%20maps%2C%20expected%20unlocked%20stage%2Fcoverage%2C%20required%20local%20assumptions%2C%20regression%20witnesses%20and%20implementation-size%20limits.%20Defer%20corpus-wide%20restoration%20that%20does%20not%20help%20the%20immediate%20pilot.%0D%0A5.%20Write%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%20with%20the%20selected%20implementation%20scope%20and%20explicit%20activation%2Fdefer%2Fstop%20decisions.%0D%0A6.%20Revise%20the%20task%20queue%20and%20add%20smaller%20standalone%20tasks%20if%20capture-section%20handling%2C%20geometry-state%20reasoning%20or%20catalogue%20representation%20exceeds%20one%20M%20deliverable.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Each%20selected%20repair%20has%20a%20specific%20original%20input%2Ferror%2C%20an%20expected%20measurable%20stage%20improvement%20and%20a%20verification%2Fexit%20condition.%0D%0A-%20Operational%20blockers%20are%20not%20misreported%20as%20evidence%20that%20aesthetic%20relighting%20is%20impossible.%0D%0A-%20Available%20static%20MP%20proof%20work%20is%20not%20delayed%20by%20restoration%20of%20the%20entire%20corpus%2C%20and%20no%20deferred%20branch%20executes%20without%20a%20decision.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20repeat%20the%20all-map%20audit%20just%20to%20rediscover%20known%20counts%2C%20treat%20a%20capture%20manifest%20entry%20as%20a%20present%20file%2C%20remove%20a%20geometry%20guard%20without%20equivalent%20scope%20checks%2C%20or%20raise%20all%20budgets%20blindly.%0D%0ADeliver%20a%20concrete%20evidence-backed%20decision%20or%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20experiment%20plus%20its%20decision.%20You%20may%20rewrite%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%20and%20future%20prompts%20or%20add%20tasks%20within%20the%20data-only%20aesthetic%20objective.%20Log%20every%20change%2C%20preserve%20prior%20frozen%20failures%2C%20and%20give%20each%20new%20task%20a%20standalone%20English%20prompt%2C%20S%2FM%20size%20and%20a%20measurable%20exit%20condition.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-37%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Record%20approved%20scope%2Fpolicy%2Fprompt%20changes%20in%20Plan%20Change%20Log%2C%20with%20fresh%20independent%20final%20evidence%20when%20old%20final%20results%20informed%20the%20change.%0D%0A) [AR-38](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-38%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Recover%20original%20captures%20and%20resolve%20input%20section%20refusals%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20the%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%2C%20concurrent%20and%20staged%20changes%2C%20including%20ASR25.%20Verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20actual%20lamps%2Fscreens%2FBSP%2Fmodel%20emission%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20an%20independently%20motivated%20probable%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20Historical%20type%2C%20XYZ%2C%20power%20and%20exactLight%20certification%20are%20not%20aesthetic%20eligibility%20requirements.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0APreserve%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20meanings.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%20and%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Record%20modern-model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ARestore%20the%20selected%20missing%20original%20observation%20inputs%20and%20diagnose%20the%20selected%20native%20section%20refusals%20through%20the%20existing%20capture%2Fexport%2Freader%20workflow%2C%20preserving%20source%20identity%20and%20format%20semantics.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%2C%20ASR25%20maps%2Fchecks%2C%20original%20assets0%E2%80%933%20BSP%20hashes%2C%20current%20capture%20manifests%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fdatasets%2F%2C%20native%20dataset%20export%2Fquality%20tools%20and%20relevant%20Offline%20read%20limits%2Fsections.%20Only%20the%20explicitly%20selected%20maps%20are%20in%20scope.%0D%0AActivation%3A%20Do%20not%20start%20implementation%20unless%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%20explicitly%20selects%20this%20branch%20and%20its%20target%20maps.%20An%20inventory%20blocker%20count%20or%20a%20completed%20decision%20file%20alone%20is%20not%20authorization%20for%20all%20repair%20branches.%20Otherwise%20retain%20Deferred%20and%20record%20the%20missing%20choice.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Verify%20original%20BSP%2Fassets%20and%20the%20actual%20missing%20or%20refused%20capture%20paths.%20Preserve%20old%20manifests%20as%20historical%20receipts%3B%20do%20not%20overwrite%20a%20valid%20input%20merely%20because%20its%20path%20changed.%0D%0A2.%20Use%20the%20existing%20capture-only%2Fexport%20workflow%20to%20restore%20or%20regenerate%20evidence%20with%20original%20map%2C%20shader%2Fmaterial%2C%20image%20and%20derived-section%20identities.%0D%0A3.%20For%20t1_rail%2Fvjun3%20or%20other%20selected%20section%20refusals%2C%20diagnose%20actual%20header%2Flayout%2Flimit%2Fversion%20behavior%20and%20apply%20a%20compatible%20bounded%20reader%2Ftool%20fix%20or%20regenerate%20with%20the%20supported%20exporter.%0D%0A4.%20Do%20not%20invent%20observations%20or%20treat%20a%20refused%20section%20as%20corruption%20without%20evidence.%20Preserve%20unsupported%20sections%20and%20precise%20external%20blockers%20when%20recovery%20is%20unavailable.%0D%0A5.%20Verify%20raw%20RGB%20bytes%2C%20geometry%2Freceiver%20mapping%2C%20section%20completeness%2C%20hashes%20and%20native%20opening%20against%20original%20BSP%20data%3B%20update%20a%20current%20availability%20manifest%20distinct%20from%20archived%20receipts.%0D%0A6.%20Demonstrate%20that%20recovered%20inputs%20reach%20the%20approved%20diagnostic%2Freconstruction%20stage%20and%20leave%20numerical%20acceptance%20unchanged%20for%20previously%20valid%20inputs.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Selected%20recoverable%20captures%20exist%20and%20pass%20native%20identity%2Fcompleteness%2Fmapping%20checks%3B%20any%20irrecoverable%20input%20has%20a%20specific%20documented%20blocker.%0D%0A-%20Section%20compatibility%20is%20fixed%20or%20causally%20resolved%20without%20changing%20canonical%20storage%20semantics%20or%20silently%20omitting%20data.%0D%0A-%20Before%2Fafter%20corpus%20availability%20and%20unlocked%20stages%20are%20reported%20separately%20from%20reconstructed-lighting%20success.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20download%20substitute%20assets%2C%20replace%20original%20observations%20with%20synthetic%20lighting%2C%20edit%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20reinterpret%20unsupported%20bytes%2C%20add%20a%20second%20capture%20format%2C%20or%20claim%20a%20restored%20file%20is%20a%20relit%20map.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20the%20existing%20real%20path%20with%20meaningful%20verification.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20sketch%2C%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20stub%20or%20report%20for%20the%20required%20integration.%20Reuse%20already%20complete%20operations.%20If%20the%20scoped%20work%20exceeds%20M%20or%20its%20required%20decision%20is%20absent%2C%20document%20the%20concrete%20split%2Fblocker%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20work%20or%20decide%20a%20new%20product%20policy.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-38%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Do%20not%20silently%20change%20acceptance%20criteria%20or%20the%20plan%20direction%3B%20propose%20a%20specific%20decision%20task%20if%20a%20branch%20is%20needed.%0D%0A) umptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AMake%20the%20selected%20original%20luminous%20catalogues%20complete%20or%20precisely%20bounded%20under%20a%20declared%20deterministic%20processing%20strategy%2C%20addressing%20the%20measured%20work%2Fpolygon%20limits%20without%20changing%20their%20geometric%20meaning.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20selected%20scope%20in%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%2C%20ASR25%20catalogue%20failures%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fbsp_fixtures%2F%20mask%20clipping%2Fconnectivity%2Funion%20code%2C%20original%20texture%20repetition%20and%20topology%2C%20and%20existing%20catalogue%20identity%20tests.%0D%0AActivation%3A%20Do%20not%20start%20implementation%20unless%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%20explicitly%20selects%20this%20branch%20and%20its%20target%20maps.%20An%20inventory%20blocker%20count%20or%20a%20completed%20decision%20file%20alone%20is%20not%20authorization%20for%20all%20repair%20branches.%20Otherwise%20retain%20Deferred%20and%20record%20the%20missing%20choice.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Profile%20the%20actual%20work%20and%20polygon%20expansion%20of%20the%20selected%20failed%20maps%20before%20choosing%20a%20repair%3B%20distinguish%20repeated%20UV%20expansion%2C%20connectivity%20comparisons%2C%20union%20topology%20and%20true%20output%20size.%0D%0A2.%20Implement%20the%20selected%20bounded%20spatial%20indexing%2C%20deduplication%2C%20deterministic%20sharding%20or%20representation%20improvement%2C%20reusing%20the%20existing%20source%20geometry%2Fmask%20semantics.%0D%0A3.%20Preserve%20cross-shard%20component%20connectivity%2C%20dark%20gaps%2C%20exact%20source%20aliases%2C%20area%2Fcentroid%20and%20global%20fixture%20identities.%20Record%20every%20shard%2C%20work%20count%20and%20completeness%20condition.%0D%0A4.%20Retain%20genuine%20unsupported%20optical%2Ftopological%20cases%20as%20unknown%3B%20no%20mask-axis%20reduction%2C%20dropped%20pieces%20or%20early%20output%20truncation%20may%20silently%20count%20as%20complete.%0D%0A5.%20Compare%20already%20supported%20catalogues%20semantically%2Fbytewise%20where%20appropriate%20and%20run%20adversarial%20repeated%2Fclamped%20UV%2C%20thin-strip%2C%20disconnected%20and%20boundary-component%20cases.%0D%0A6.%20Execute%20the%20approved%20difficult-map%20set%20and%20report%20completed%20catalogue%20coverage%2C%20remaining%20blockers%2C%20resource%20measurements%20and%20the%20stages%20actually%20unlocked.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20selected%20processing%20mechanism%20is%20implemented%20and%20integrated%2C%20with%20independently%20checked%20area%2Fcomponent%20semantics%20and%20bounded%20repeatable%20work.%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20the%20targeted%20causal%20failure%20class%20has%20measured%20before%2Fafter%20evidence%3B%20any%20remaining%20budget%2Frepresentation%20refusal%20stays%20explicit%20and%20cannot%20publish%20a%20complete-catalogue%20claim.%0D%0A-%20Catalogue%20completion%20is%20reported%20as%20input%20preparation%2C%20not%20accepted%20source%20or%20map%20reconstruction.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20solve%20the%20audit%20by%20globally%20increasing%20limits%20without%20profiling%2C%20silently%20simplifying%20luminous%20geometry%2C%20changing%20historical%20extraction%20results%2C%20merging%20unrelated%20lamps%2C%20or%20publishing%20partial%20shards%20as%20a%20finished%20catalogue.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20the%20existing%20real%20path%20with%20meaningful%20verification.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20sketch%2C%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20stub%20or%20report%20for%20the%20required%20integration.%20Reuse%20already%20complete%20operations.%20If%20the%20scoped%20work%20exceeds%20M%20or%20its%20required%20decision%20is%20absent%2C%20document%20the%20concrete%20split%2Fblocker%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20work%20or%20decide%20a%20new%20product%20policy.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-39%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Do%20not%20silently%20change%20acceptance%20criteria%20or%20the%20plan%20direction%3B%20propose%20a%20specific%20decision%20task%20if%20a%20branch%20is%20needed.%0D%0A) [AR-40](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-40%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Replace%20the%20whole-map%20dynamic%20veto%20with%20scoped%20evidence%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20the%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%2C%20concurrent%20and%20staged%20changes%2C%20including%20ASR25.%20Verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20actual%20lamps%2Fscreens%2FBSP%2Fmodel%20emission%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20an%20independently%20motivated%20probable%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20Historical%20type%2C%20XYZ%2C%20power%20and%20exactLight%20certification%20are%20not%20aesthetic%20eligibility%20requirements.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0APreserve%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20meanings.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%20and%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Record%20modern-model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%2C%20bounded%20approximations%20and%20assistance.%20Never%20promote%20a%20conditional%20lamp%20or%20processed%20map%20to%20historical%20truth%20or%20complete%20reconstruction.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0APermit%20the%20specifically%20approved%20locally%20supportable%20regions%20of%20maps%20containing%20movers%20to%20use%20the%20existing%20aesthetic%20pipeline%2C%20while%20preserving%20unknown%20motion%2Focclusion%20and%20required-path%20safety.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%20and%20its%20chosen%20local-state%20proof%2Fassumption%20contract%2C%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_aesthetic%2Fdiscover.py%2C%20the%20native%20grid%20bridge%2C%20SourceSupport%2C%20PointRefine%20occupancy%2C%20captured%20model%20bounds%2Fstates%20and%20ASR25%20DynamicMapUnsupported%20cases.%0D%0AActivation%3A%20Do%20not%20start%20implementation%20unless%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fcorpus-blocker-decision.md%20explicitly%20selects%20this%20branch%20and%20its%20target%20maps.%20An%20inventory%20blocker%20count%20or%20a%20completed%20decision%20file%20alone%20is%20not%20authorization%20for%20all%20repair%20branches.%20Otherwise%20retain%20Deferred%20and%20record%20the%20missing%20choice.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20artifacts.%20A%20missing%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20a%20precise%20blocker%2C%20not%20permission%20to%20invent%20it%3B%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20This%20prompt%20requires%20no%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Separate%20static-world%20fixture%20identity%2C%20local%20source%20support%2C%20receiver%20paths%20and%20mover%2Fstate%20uncertainty%20instead%20of%20rejecting%20every%20candidate%20from%20any%20map%20with%20dynamic_models%20metadata.%0D%0A2.%20Implement%20only%20the%20selected%20evidence-backed%20local%20policy%20using%20existing%20BSP%2C%20visual%20geometry%20and%20occupancy%20providers.%20Unknown%20travel%20extents%20cannot%20be%20bounded%20by%20captured%20pose%20alone.%0D%0A3.%20Require%20an%20actual%20certificate%20or%20explicitly%20approved%20conditional%20scene-state%20hypothesis%20for%20every%20admitted%20scope.%20Label%20conditional%20assumptions%3B%20an%20assumption%20is%20not%20an%20independently%20known-clear%20ray.%0D%0A4.%20Keep%20unavailable%20required%20paths%20unavailable%20and%20reject%20independently%20known%20solid%20intersections%2Funknown%20optical%20geometry.%20Retain%20the%20old%20strict%20path%20and%20historical%20behavior.%0D%0A5.%20Scope%20cache%2Fprovenance%2Fcoverage%20to%20the%20chosen%20state%20interpretation%20and%20verify%20moved%2Funknown-door%2C%20far-static-fixture%2C%20same-room%20occlusion%20and%20stale-state%20counterexamples.%0D%0A6.%20Run%20the%20approved%20affected-map%20regions%20and%20report%20newly%20examinable%20support%2C%20conditional%20coverage%2C%20unchanged%20rejections%20and%20any%20remaining%20physical%2Finput%20blocker.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20global%20controller%20veto%20is%20replaced%20only%20where%20equivalent%20local%20evidence%2Fexplicit%20policy%20exists%3B%20required%20geometry%20and%20occupancy%20checks%20still%20decide%20admission.%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20one%20approved%20local%20scope%20reaches%20the%20intended%20next%20stage%2C%20or%20the%20measured%20lack%20of%20the%20required%20evidence%20is%20explicitly%20returned%20to%20a%20decision%20instead%20of%20bypassed.%0D%0A-%20Newly%20processed%20dynamic-map%20scopes%20are%20not%20claimed%20historically%20bake-correct%2C%20unconditionally%20free%20or%20completely%20reconstructed.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20scoped%20artifact%2C%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20remove%20the%20dynamic%20guard%20unconditionally%2C%20use%20a%20current%20AABB%20as%20an%20unknown%20mover%27s%20full%20travel%20envelope%2C%20ignore%20doors%2Fmodels%20in%20visibility%2C%20mark%20conditional%20assumptions%20as%20proven%20empty%20geometry%2C%20or%20activate%20runtime%20lighting.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20the%20existing%20real%20path%20with%20meaningful%20verification.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20sketch%2C%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20stub%20or%20report%20for%20the%20required%20integration.%20Reuse%20already%20complete%20operations.%20If%20the%20scoped%20work%20exceeds%20M%20or%20its%20required%20decision%20is%20absent%2C%20document%20the%20concrete%20split%2Fblocker%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20work%20or%20decide%20a%20new%20product%20policy.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md%20and%20append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-40%22%20in%20Results%20Log%2C%20including%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%20and%20assistance%2C%20result%2C%20measured%20evidence%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20limitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20A%20completed%20investigation%20can%20make%20a%20valid%20negative%20decision%3B%20it%20cannot%20turn%20a%20failed%20product%20gate%20into%20Passed.%20Do%20not%20claim%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20Do%20not%20silently%20change%20acceptance%20criteria%20or%20the%20plan%20direction%3B%20propose%20a%20specific%20decision%20task%20if%20a%20branch%20is%20needed.%0D%0A) [AR-24](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-24%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Plan%20complete%20map%20work%20from%20original%20geometry%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ACreate%20a%20bounded%20scene-wide%20work%20graph%20that%20covers%20an%20entire%20original%20stock%20map%20and%20supplies%20reproducible%20local%20fitting%20jobs%20without%20choosing%20regions%20by%20favorable%20RGB%20results.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20SceneSchedule%2Fscene_schedule.%2A%2C%20region%20graphs%2C%20original%20receiver%2Ffixture%20inventories%2C%20current%20stock_aesthetic%20orchestration%2C%20and%20the%20frozen%20aesthetic%20map-coverage%20policy.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20tools%2Frend2%2Fstock_audit%2F%20already%20accounts%20for%20all%2057%20BSPs%20and%20measured%20blockers.%20Import%20its%20census%2Fprovenance%2C%20then%20add%20actual%20scene%20work%2Faccepted-area%20accounting%3B%20do%20not%20repeat%20inventory%20processing%20and%20label%20it%20whole-map%20reconstruction.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Partition%20original%20static%20geometry%20into%20meaningful%20connected%20regions%20and%20overlap%2Fboundary%20scopes%2C%20with%20stable%20IDs%20and%20a%20fixed%20eligible-area%20census.%0D%0A2.%20Assign%20fixture%20components%2C%20global%20hypotheses%2C%20C%20support%20and%20original%20receiver%20samples%20to%20jobs%20while%20retaining%20shared%20sources%20and%20cross-region%20visibility.%0D%0A3.%20Generate%20spatial%2Fmaterial%2Fnormal-balanced%20training%2C%20validation%20and%20final%20support%20before%20fitting%2C%20with%20footprint%2Ffilter%20margins%20and%20explicit%20correlation%20handling.%0D%0A4.%20Represent%20missing%20models%2C%20unknown%20geometry%2C%20unsupported%20materials%2C%20dark%20regions%20and%20no-source%20regions%20as%20first-class%20jobs%2Foutcomes.%0D%0A5.%20Bound%20scheduling%20and%20memory%20without%20coupling%20source%20selection%20to%20renderer%20limits%3B%20distinguish%20budget-exhausted%20work%20from%20completed%20empty%20results.%0D%0A6.%20Export%20an%20inspectable%20map%20work%20graph%2C%20all%20denominators%2C%20adjacency%2Foverlap%20records%20and%20deterministic%20local%20request%20manifests.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Every%20eligible%20original%20region%20belongs%20to%20a%20documented%20work%20scope%2C%20including%20unsupported%20and%20no-light%20cases.%0D%0A-%20Jobs%20and%20evaluation%20membership%20are%20target-independent%20and%20reproducible%20across%20order%20changes.%0D%0A-%20A%20map%20cannot%20appear%20complete%20merely%20because%20only%20easy%20regions%20were%20scheduled.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20discover%20rooms%20from%20fitted%20residuals%20alone%2C%20drop%20failed%20jobs%20from%20the%20map%20denominator%2C%20impose%20a%20future%20runtime%20light%20budget%2C%20or%20create%20a%20second%20independent%20region%20identity%20system%20unnecessarily.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-24%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-25](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-25%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Implementation%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Reconcile%20shared%20sources%20and%20lighting%20across%20map%20regions%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ACombine%20regional%20fits%20into%20one%20consistent%20map-level%20data%20configuration%20with%20stable%20sources%2C%20a%20common%20brightness%20gauge%20and%20explicit%20unresolved%20boundaries.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20complete%20map%20work%20graph%2C%20authoritative%20aesthetic%20fitter%2C%20source%2Ffixture%20lineage%2C%20global%2FC%20models%2C%20source%20contributions%20and%20existing%20joint%2Fscene%20orchestration.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Identify%20shared%20physical%20fixtures%20by%20attested%20aliases%2Finstance%2Fcomponent%20identity%2C%20preserving%20genuinely%20separate%20nearby%20lights%20and%20mutually%20exclusive%20variants.%0D%0A2.%20Resolve%20overlapping%20regional%20proposals%20into%20consistent%20source%20membership%20and%20refit%20shared%20RGB%2Fglobal%20parameters%20on%20the%20combined%20training%20support%20with%20the%20fixed%20gauge.%0D%0A3.%20Use%20bounded%20connected-component%20and%20overlap%20refits%20with%20explicit%20shared-parameter%20dependencies%3B%20do%20not%20require%20a%20new%20monolithic%20whole-map%20optimizer.%0D%0A4.%20Propagate%20cross-room%20source%20influence%20and%20respect%20walls%2Fopenings%3B%20quantify%20boundary%20disagreement%2C%20C%20continuity%20and%20region-specific%20coverage.%0D%0A5.%20Prevent%20room-by-room%20exposure%2C%20background%20or%20transfer%20choices%20from%20hiding%20seams%20or%20creating%20incompatible%20powers%20for%20the%20same%20source.%0D%0A6.%20Retain%20competing%20map%20configurations%20and%20D%20boundaries%20when%20the%20evidence%20cannot%20resolve%20a%20conflict.%0D%0A7.%20Validate%20adjacent%20warm%2Fcool%20rooms%2C%20a%20shared%20corridor%20fixture%2C%20a%20doorway%2C%20overlapping%20region%20schedules%20and%20a%20multiroom%20stock%20candidate.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20One%20map-level%20source%2Fconfiguration%20identity%20reproduces%20all%20accepted%20regional%20fits%20within%20the%20declared%20consistency%20tolerances.%0D%0A-%20Duplicate%20sources%20and%20brightness%20seams%20have%20explicit%20measured%20dispositions.%0D%0A-%20Unresolved%20joins%20remain%20Partial%2FD%20and%20are%20not%20concealed%20by%20independent%20room%20exposures.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20concatenate%20local%20light%20lists%20and%20call%20the%20map%20solved%2C%20merge%20by%20proximity%2Fcolor%20only%2C%20smooth%20fields%20across%20opaque%20walls%2C%20or%20use%20held-out%20room%20data%20to%20repair%20training%20parameters.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-25%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) ded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AChoose%20a%20complete%20representation%20of%20aesthetic%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20decisions%20and%20modern%20model%20policy%20using%20existing%20canonical%20contracts%20plus%20explicit%20offline%20metadata%2C%20without%20conflating%20aesthetic%20usability%20with%20historical%20certainty.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tr_lightrecon_types.%2A%2C%20lightrecon%2F%7Bdisk%2Cproducer%2Ccontributions%2Cfield_builder%2Cfield_codec%2Cshape_selection%7D.%2A%2C%20current%20calibration%2Fconditional-field%20contracts%2C%20frozen%20modern-lighting%20and%20coverage%20policies%2C%20and%20a%20reviewed%20fitted%20region.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Map%20A%2FB%20sources%20to%20existing%20PhysicalEmitter%2FCandidate%2C%20geometry%2C%20relative%20photometry%2C%20provenance%2C%20configuration%20and%20alternatives%20without%20requiring%20exactLight%20or%20fabricating%20measured%20existence%20confidence.%0D%0A2.%20Map%20C%20to%20a%20faithful%20supported%20conditional%20field%2Ftransport%20quantity%20and%20D%20to%20explicit%20original-observation%2Ffallback%20references%20with%20region%2Freceiver%20scope.%0D%0A3.%20Specify%20the%20saved%20modern-law%2Fradius%2Frange%20policy%2C%20emitting%20footprint%20aliases%2C%20assistance%2C%20assumptions%2C%20coverage%2C%20quality%20receipts%20and%20input%20dependencies.%0D%0A4.%20Use%20a%20versioned%20offline%20sidecar%2Fmanifest%20for%20aesthetic%20decisions%20and%20model%20details%20that%20are%20not%20canonical%20physical%20quantities.%20Preserve%20all%20current%20canonical%20type%2Fcodec%20semantics.%0D%0A5.%20Test%20whether%20an%20existing%20format%20faithfully%20carries%20every%20required%20value.%20If%20a%20genuine%20semantic%20gap%20remains%2C%20document%20it%20and%20add%20a%20narrowly%20scoped%20decision%2Fimplementation%20task%20rather%20than%20stuffing%20data%20into%20unrelated%20fields.%0D%0A6.%20Publish%20a%20readable%20data%20contract%2C%20machine-readable%20schema%2Fvalidation%20examples%2C%20invalid%20counterexamples%20and%20a%20backward-compatibility%20decision.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20All%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20outcomes%20and%20selected%2Fequivalent%20configurations%20have%20precise%20data%20representations%20and%20calibration%2Funcertainty%20semantics.%0D%0A-%20Saving%20an%20aesthetically%20usable%20candidate%20does%20not%20mark%20it%20historically%20Resolved%20or%20bypass%20canonical%20validation.%0D%0A-%20Existing%20assets%20remain%20interpretable%2C%20and%20a%20consumer-free%20replay%20can%20identify%20exactly%20which%20modern%20policy%20produced%20the%20result.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20extend%20canonical%20schemas%20casually%2C%20overload%20rangeWorld%20or%20exactLight%20with%20new%20meanings%2C%20invent%20a%20second%20physical-light%20taxonomy%2C%20or%20design%20a%20renderer%20replacement%2Fdouble-lighting%20contract.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-27%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) ncoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AImplement%20the%20complete%20saved-data%20path%20and%20standalone%20replay%20validator%20for%20the%20accepted%20aesthetic%20contract%2C%20without%20producing%20or%20activating%20runtime%20lights.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20aesthetic%20output%20schema%2Fdecision%2C%20existing%20Producer%2Fdisk%2Fchunk%2Fcalibration%20APIs%2C%20conditional%20field%20codecs%2C%20contribution%20records%20and%20complete%20reviewed%20fit%20artifacts.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Serialize%20selected%20A%2FB%20source%20parameters%2C%20actual%20footprint%2Fsupport%20references%2C%20global%2FC%20results%2C%20D%20references%2C%20alternative%20configurations%20and%20modern%20model-policy%20identity.%0D%0A2.%20Preserve%20stable%20source%2Ffixture%2Fregion%20lineage%20and%20explicit%20relative%20quantities%2C%20assumptions%2C%20assistance%20and%20review%20scope.%0D%0A3.%20Write%20immutable%20dependency%20manifests%20and%20content%20hashes%20for%20BSP%2C%20shaders%2C%20images%2C%20models%2C%20observations%2C%20policies%20and%20derived%20geometry.%0D%0A4.%20Validate%20all%20references%2C%20finite%2Fbounded%20parameters%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20domains%2C%20source-family%20exclusivity%2C%20coverage%20totals%20and%20canonical%20invariants%20before%20publishing%20files.%0D%0A5.%20Implement%20standalone%20load%2Freplay%20of%20predictions%20and%20diagnostic%20views%20from%20the%20saved%20data%3B%20do%20not%20require%20optimizer%20internals%20or%20hidden%20answer%20files.%0D%0A6.%20Test%20round%20trips%2C%20stale%20inputs%2C%20unsupported%20versions%2C%20corrupt%20chunks%2C%20missing%20footprints%2C%20alias%20collisions%20and%20equivalence%2Funknown%20records.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20complete%20fitted%20region%2Fmap%20can%20be%20saved%2C%20validated%2C%20reloaded%20and%20reproduce%20reviewed%20numerical%20predictions%20within%20frozen%20tolerances.%0D%0A-%20The%20data%20contains%20both%20successful%20reconstruction%20and%20explicit%20C%2FD%2Funsupported%20scope%3B%20no%20missing%20piece%20is%20silently%20invented.%0D%0A-%20No%20renderer%20state%2C%20GPU%20object%2C%20BSP%20edit%20or%20in-game%20light%20activation%20occurs.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20deliver%20only%20diagnostic%20JSON%20that%20cannot%20reproduce%20the%20selected%20model%2C%20bypass%20canonical%20validators%2C%20or%20mark%20export%20success%20as%20visual%20acceptance.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-28%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) B8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AAccept%2C%20partially%20accept%20or%20reject%20one%20whole%20original%20stock%20map%20as%20a%20data-only%20aesthetic%20relighting%20deliverable%20under%20frozen%20coverage%20and%20visual-quality%20gates.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20map%20acceptance%20policy%2C%20original%20inventory%2Fcensus%2C%20complete%20map%20work%20graph%2C%20consistent%20fitted%20configuration%2C%20export%20validator%20and%20offline%20review%20packet.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Freeze%20the%20map%20configuration%20and%20all%20assumptions%20before%20evaluating%20its%20independent%20final%20support.%0D%0A2.%20Review%20every%20declared%20room%2Fregion%2C%20major%20color%20motif%2C%20visible-fixture%20attachment%2C%20dark%20control%2C%20exterior%2Finterior%20transition%20and%20unresolved%20high-salience%20location.%0D%0A3.%20Report%20reconstructed%20A%2FB%20coverage%2C%20independently%20supported%20C%20coverage%2C%20D%2Foriginal%20fallback%2C%20unsupported%20geometry%20and%20execution%20completeness%20separately.%0D%0A4.%20Perform%20source-removal%2C%20alternative-shape%2Fbackground%20and%20sensitivity%20checks%20on%20development%2Fvalidation%20evidence%3B%20report%20final%20scores%20without%20refitting.%0D%0A5.%20Verify%20the%20saved%20data%20can%20be%20loaded%20and%20reproduce%20the%20reviewed%20predictions%20without%20the%20fitting%20process%20or%20original%20optimizer%20state.%0D%0A6.%20Write%20a%20map%20acceptance%20record%20with%20stable%20release%20identity%2C%20limitations%20and%20concrete%20repair%20tasks.%20A%20failed%20or%20partial%20map%20cannot%20be%20relabeled%20complete.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20one%20map%20has%20a%20substantive%20accepted%20data-only%20result%20or%20a%20precise%20failed%2Fpartial%20decision%20with%20all%20original%20coverage%20retained.%0D%0A-%20Success%20does%20not%20depend%20on%20unknown%20historical%20positions%2C%20source%20count%20or%20SI%20powers.%0D%0A-%20A%20reviewer%20can%20reproduce%20the%20accepted%20scene%20from%20saved%20data%20and%20fixed%20display%2Fappearance%20settings.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20report%20one%20successful%20room%20as%20an%20entire%20map%2C%20treat%20original-lighting%20fallback%20as%20newly%20reconstructed%20area%2C%20modify%20the%20BSP%20or%20activate%20relighting%20in%20the%20game.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-26%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) 20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ARun%20and%20resume%20original-stock%20reconstruction%20jobs%20reliably%20across%20maps%20with%20immutable%20inputs%2C%20correct%20invalidation%20and%20bounded%20offline%20resources.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20tools%2Frend2%2Fall_stock%2F%2C%20current%20stock_aesthetic%20and%20aesthetic_relighting%20orchestration%2C%20scene%20scheduling%2C%20prepared%20caches%2C%20dependency%20manifests%20and%20the%20validated%20output%2Freplay%20contract.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20reuse%20the%20existing%20two-worker%20stock_audit%20controller%2C%20frozen%20policies%20and%20per-stage%20receipts.%20Its%2057-map%20audit%20is%20not%20a%20resumable%20accepted-reconstruction%20pipeline%2C%20and%20its%20old%20capture%20manifest%20does%20not%20prove%20current%20file%20availability.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Create%20a%20deterministic%20batch%20runner%20over%20a%20frozen%20original%20inventory%20with%20per-map%2Fper-region%20preparation%2C%20fitting%2C%20validation%2C%20review-packet%20and%20export%20states.%0D%0A2.%20Cache%20only%20compatible%20geometry%2Fresponse%20data%2C%20keyed%20by%20map%2Fassets%2C%20emitting%20support%2C%20modern%20model%2C%20pose%2Fsize%2Frange%2C%20material%2Fappearance%20policy%20and%20occupancy%20assumptions.%0D%0A3.%20Resume%20interrupted%20jobs%20without%20reusing%20stale%20target-dependent%20state%20or%20mixing%20policy%20revisions%3B%20preserve%20failed%20attempts%20and%20source%20histories.%0D%0A4.%20Distinguish%20Completed%2C%20Partial%2C%20Failed%2C%20Blocked%2C%20Budget%20and%20MissingInput%20from%20Accepted%2C%20and%20aggregate%20all%20coverage%20denominators.%0D%0A5.%20Record%20time%2FRSS%2Fwork%20separately%20from%20quality%3B%20support%20bounded%20sharding%20without%20future%20renderer%20limits%20or%20silent%20truncation.%0D%0A6.%20Test%20interruption%2C%20changed%20textures%2Fmodels%2Fpolicies%2C%20reordered%20jobs%2C%20corrupted%20artifacts%20and%20exact%20repeat%2Fresume%20equivalence.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20A%20multi-map%20run%20can%20resume%20safely%20and%20produces%20the%20same%20semantic%20results%20as%20a%20clean%20run%20under%20the%20same%20frozen%20policy.%0D%0A-%20Changed%20inputs%20invalidate%20all%20affected%20downstream%20artifacts%20while%20unaffected%20work%20is%20reused.%0D%0A-%20Corpus%20reports%20retain%20every%20map%20and%20failed%2Funknown%20region%20rather%20than%20only%20successful%20outputs.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20call%20a%20finished%20process%20an%20accepted%20reconstruction%2C%20reuse%20caches%20across%20different%20modern%20laws%20or%20masks%2C%20delete%20earlier%20failures%2C%20or%20optimize%20quality%20by%20dropping%20expensive%20regions.%0D%0AImplement%20the%20specified%20mechanism%20completely%20through%20its%20real%20native%2Forchestration%20path%2C%20with%20meaningful%20regression%20and%20reference%20checks.%20Do%20not%20substitute%20a%20loose%20conceptual%20approximation%2C%20a%20disconnected%20helper%2C%20a%20stub%20or%20a%20report%20for%20working%20integration.%20If%20the%20work%20is%20larger%20than%20M%2C%20complete%20the%20bounded%20independent%20work%20and%20document%20a%20concrete%20split%20for%20a%20decision%3B%20do%20not%20silently%20expand%20the%20ticket.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-29%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20implementation%20task%20may%20update%20its%20own%20status%2C%20evidence%20and%20result%20summary%20but%20must%20not%20silently%20rewrite%20product%20scope%2C%20acceptance%20gates%2C%20the%20roadmap%20or%20other%20prompts.%20Record%20proposed%20changes%20for%20an%20explicit%20decision.%0D%0A) [AR-30](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-30%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Decision%20%2F%20Experiment%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Validate%20transfer%20to%20a%20representative%20stock-map%20bank%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADetermine%20whether%20the%20first-map%20solution%20generalizes%20to%20stock%20scenes%20with%20different%20visual%20and%20geometric%20characteristics%20before%20large-scale%20acceptance.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20original%20map%20inventory%2C%20accepted-map%20receipts%2C%20current%20failure%20taxonomy%2C%20fixed%20model%2Fpolicies%20and%20batch%20runner.%20Use%20existing%20stock%20coverage%20reports%20to%20choose%20representative%20cases%20before%20final%20scoring.%0D%0ACurrent%20evidence%20update%3A%20ASR25%20already%20provides%20210%20original-observation%20ROIs%20on%2035%20maps%20and%20a%20failure%20packet.%20They%20are%20development%20evidence%20for%20designing%20this%20bank%2C%20not%20untouched%20final%20tests.%20Preserve%20the%20two%20accepted%20bounded%20source%20examples%20and%20all%20rejected%20scopes.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Freeze%20a%20development%2Fvalidation%2Ffinal%20map%20bank%20covering%20colored%20interiors%2C%20outdoor%20sun%2Fsky%2C%20screens%2Fpanels%2C%20strips%2Ftubes%2C%20static%20model%20fixtures%2C%20mixed%20materials%2C%20complex%20occlusion%20and%20mover%20uncertainty.%0D%0A2.%20Apply%20the%20same%20modern%20policy%20and%20assistance%20rules%3B%20identify%20any%20allowed%20per-map%20artistic%20settings%20in%20advance%20rather%20than%20fitting%20unrestricted%20exposure.%0D%0A3.%20Evaluate%20map%20and%20area%20coverage%2C%20high-salience%20failures%2C%20A%2FB%20plausibility%2C%20C%20quality%2C%20D%20budgets%2C%20shadows%20and%20visual%20composition%20with%20the%20same%20acceptance%20procedure.%0D%0A4.%20Separate%20automatic%20and%20assisted%20results%20and%20document%20manual%20effort%20by%20map%2Fcategory.%0D%0A5.%20Choose%20which%20specific%20capability%20gaps%20merit%20implementation%20and%20which%20can%20honestly%20remain%20documented%20C%2FD%20within%20the%20accepted%20product%20scope.%0D%0A6.%20Update%20the%20plan%20and%20concrete%20prompts%20from%20measured%20failures%3B%20create%20a%20fresh%20final%20bank%20if%20final%20results%20informed%20changes.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20The%20generalization%20decision%20is%20based%20on%20representative%20original%20maps%20and%20frozen%20criteria%2C%20not%20repeated%20tuning%20of%20the%20first%20successful%20scene.%0D%0A-%20Each%20unsupported%20scene%20class%20has%20a%20concrete%20implementation%2Fdecision%20disposition.%0D%0A-%20The%20next%20corpus%20milestone%20has%20explicit%20policy%2C%20assistance%20and%20coverage%20commitments.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20substitute%20controlled%20compiled%20scenes%20for%20stock%20evidence%2C%20report%20only%20favorable%20maps%2C%20treat%20original%20source%20truth%20as%20available%2C%20or%20hide%20per-map%20manual%20corrections.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-30%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A)
