%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0AEstablish%20accepted%20aesthetic%20relighting%20for%20at%20least%20half%20the%20frozen%20original-stock%20map%20inventory%20while%20reporting%20complete%20corpus%20execution%20and%20spatial%20coverage.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20frozen%20original%20inventory%2C%20acceptance%20policy%2C%20representative-map%20decision%2C%20validated%20batch%2Fexport%20tools%20and%20per-map%2Fregion%20results.%20The%20historical%20local%20inventory%20has%2057%20maps%3B%20verify%20the%20current%20frozen%20denominator%20and%20override%20rules.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Freeze%20the%20milestone%20map%20set%20and%20acceptance%20criteria%20before%20the%20final%20evaluation.%20For%20a%2057-map%20inventory%2C%20at%20least%2029%20maps%20must%20actually%20pass%2C%20not%20merely%20execute.%0D%0A2.%20Run%20every%20selected%20map%20with%20the%20declared%20assistance%20policy%20and%20full%20original%20region%20census%3B%20keep%20unsuccessful%20and%20unprocessed%20maps%20visible.%0D%0A3.%20Report%20accepted%20maps%2C%20A%2FB%20reconstructed%20area%2C%20C%20supported%20field%20area%2C%20D%2Foriginal%20fallback%2C%20unknown%20geometry%20and%20high-salience%20exceptions%20separately.%0D%0A4.%20Audit%20source%20provenance%2C%20duplicate%20fixtures%2C%20wall%20leakage%2C%20color%20motifs%2C%20region%20outliers%2C%20round-trip%20replay%20and%20repeatability%20on%20a%20stratified%20sample.%0D%0A5.%20Classify%20remaining%20failures%20by%20cause%20and%20expected%20repair%20scope%20rather%20than%20promising%20a%20percentage%20from%20candidate%20counts.%0D%0A6.%20Issue%20a%20milestone%20decision%20and%20revise%20remaining%20tasks%2Fprompts%20with%20concrete%20S%2FM%20repairs%20and%20honest%20scope%20implications.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20At%20least%20half%20the%20frozen%20maps%20pass%20their%20whole-map%20aesthetic%2Fdata%20gates%2C%20or%20the%20report%20explicitly%20records%20that%20the%20milestone%20failed.%0D%0A-%20A%20map%20count%20cannot%20hide%20poor%20area%20coverage%20or%20excessive%20original-lighting%20fallback.%0D%0A-%20The%20remaining-map%20plan%20names%20substantive%20blockers%2C%20measurable%20next%20experiments%20and%20bounded%20implementation%20work.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20count%20processed%20maps%2C%20admitted%20candidates%2C%20copied%20original%20lighting%20or%20successful%20regions%20as%20accepted%20whole%20maps.%20Do%20not%20change%20the%20inventory%20denominator%20after%20seeing%20failures.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-31%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) ons%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ATurn%20the%20remaining%20original-map%20failures%20into%20a%20finite%2C%20prioritized%20repair%20program%20and%20decide%20which%20documented%20limitations%20are%20acceptable%20for%20the%20final%20data-only%20product.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20current%20all-map%20failure%2Fcoverage%20reports%2C%20frozen%20acceptance%20and%20output%20contracts%2C%20rejected%20visual%20packets%20and%20the%20implemented%20original-geometry%2Fmaterial%2Fsource%20adapters.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Group%20remaining%20failures%20into%20exact-footprint%2Fmaterial%20support%2C%20model%2Fmover%20participation%2C%20curved%2Funknown%20geometry%2C%20source%20selection%2C%20global%2FC%20attribution%2C%20numerical%20scaling%20or%20genuinely%20insufficient%20evidence.%0D%0A2.%20For%20each%20group%20choose%20a%20bounded%20repair%2C%20a%20targeted%20experiment%2C%20an%20explicitly%20assisted%20correction%20tied%20to%20original%20fixtures%2C%20or%20a%20C%2FD%20disposition%20consistent%20with%20product%20gates.%0D%0A3.%20Create%20fully%20standalone%20English%20prompts%20for%20concrete%20new%20tasks%2C%20each%20with%20type%2C%20S%2FM%20size%2C%20inputs%2C%20action%20plan%2C%20DoD%2C%20non-goals%20and%20mandatory%20plan-result%20append.%20Split%20anything%20estimated%20L%2FXL%20before%20execution.%0D%0A4.%20Preserve%20every%20failed%20run%2C%20original%20criterion%20and%20assistance%20distinction.%20If%20acceptance%20policy%20changes%2C%20document%20the%20rationale%20and%20evaluate%20on%20fresh%20independent%20scopes.%0D%0A5.%20Set%20an%20exit%20decision%20for%20every%20remaining%20group%3B%20speculative%20research%20without%20a%20measurable%20product%20decision%20is%20not%20a%20completion%20path.%0D%0A6.%20Update%20the%20plan%2C%20dependency%20table%2C%20result%20ledger%20and%20final-corpus%20acceptance%20inputs.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20No%20remaining%20failure%20is%20hidden%20behind%20a%20generic%20%27improve%20the%20solver%27%20task.%0D%0A-%20Every%20proposed%20change%20has%20evidence%2C%20a%20bounded%20deliverable%20and%20a%20measurable%20exit%20condition.%0D%0A-%20Any%20allowed%20C%2FD%20limitation%20is%20visible%20in%20final%20coverage%20and%20cannot%20convert%20a%20substantially%20incomplete%20map%20into%20a%20complete%20one.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20author%20unmotivated%20extra%20lights%20to%20make%20a%20map%20pass%2C%20indefinitely%20increase%20model%2Fbackground%20flexibility%2C%20invent%20unavailable%20geometry%2C%20or%20declare%20all%20maps%20complete%20by%20relabeling%20failures%20as%20artistic%20choices.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-32%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A) [AR-33](codex://threads/new?path=C%3A%2FUsers%2FKeyten%2FDesktop%2Fprojects%2FOpenJK-rend2&prompt=Task%20ID%3A%20AR-33%0D%0ATask%20type%3A%20Decision%20%2F%20Experiment%0D%0AEstimated%20size%3A%20M%0D%0ATitle%3A%20Accept%20the%20complete%20original-stock%20relighting%20dataset%0D%0A%0D%0ARepository%20and%20working%20rules%3A%0D%0AWork%20in%20the%20supplied%20OpenJK-rend2%20checkout%20for%20the%20keyten%2FJedi-Knight-2026%20project%20on%20the%20active%20Rend2%20development%20branch%20%28historically%20rend2-unified-wip%29.%20Inspect%20current%20code%2C%20git%20status%2C%20relevant%20AGENTS.md%20instructions%20and%20actual%20revision%2Fremote%20before%20editing.%20Preserve%20unrelated%20and%20concurrent%20changes%3B%20verify%20and%20reuse%20newer%20completed%20work.%0D%0A%0D%0AProduct%20objective%3A%0D%0AProduce%20data-only%20Aesthetic%20Relighting%20of%20original%20stock%20maps%3A%20a%20compact%20set%20of%20visually%20motivated%20modern%20sources%20attached%20to%20real%20lamps%2C%20screens%2C%20luminous%20BSP%2Fmodel%20geometry%2C%20plus%20justified%20nonlocal%20illumination.%20Original%20lighting%20is%20a%20visual%20reference%2C%20not%20a%20literal%20historical%20inverse%20problem.%20A%20means%20a%20visibly%20supported%20fixture%2C%20B%20a%20plausible%20independently%20motivated%20unseen%20source%2C%20C%20supported%20illumination%20distribution%20without%20an%20identified%20lamp%2C%20and%20D%20unresolved%2Fconflicting%20evidence%20with%20original-data%20fallback.%20A%2FB%20aesthetic%20suitability%20is%20independent%20of%20historical%20exactLight%20certification.%0D%0A%0D%0AMandatory%20boundaries%3A%0D%0AKeep%20historical%20solver%20behavior%20and%20existing%20canonical%2Fstorage%20semantics%20intact.%20Do%20not%20modify%20BSP%2Flightmaps%2C%20author%20unmotivated%20new%20lights%2C%20implement%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%20or%20world%20shading%2C%20or%20solve%20double%20lighting.%20Unknown%20geometry%20is%20not%20empty%3B%20unavailable%20predictions%20are%20not%20black.%20Encoded%20RGB8%20is%20not%20calibrated%20linear%20irradiance.%20Save%20model%2Ftransfer%2Fgauge%20assumptions%20and%20provenance%3B%20never%20promote%20a%20hypothetical%20lamp%20to%20historical%20truth.%20Approved%20bounded%20approximations%20must%20be%20explicit%20and%20validated.%0D%0A%0D%0AObjective%3A%0D%0ADeliver%20and%20audit%20the%20final%20data-only%20aesthetic%20relighting%20dataset%20for%20the%20entire%20frozen%20stock-map%20corpus%20with%20explicit%20per-map%20quality%2C%20coverage%20and%20residual%20limitations.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20inputs%20and%20inspection%3A%0D%0AInspect%20the%20original%20inventory%2C%20final%20acceptance%2Fassistance%20policies%2C%20all%20map%2Fregion%20receipts%2C%20validated%20exports%2C%20replay%20tools%20and%20every%20pending%2Fblocked%20plan%20item.%20Use%20the%20current%20actual%20code%20and%20frozen%20artifact%20identities.%0D%0AExecution%20authorization%3A%20This%20is%20a%20post-viability%20roadmap%20item.%20Read%20docs%2Frend2-relighting%2Fviability-verdict.md%20and%20the%20current%20approved%20branch%20before%20substantive%20work.%20Proceed%20only%20when%20that%20decision%20explicitly%20needs%20this%20deliverable%20or%20authorizes%20a%20bounded%20research%2Frepair%20scope.%20A%20completed%20negative%20decision%20is%20not%20Go.%20If%20the%20verdict%20is%20absent%2C%20Stop%2C%20or%20does%20not%20authorize%20the%20scope%2C%20retain%20the%20work%20as%20Deferred%2FBlocked%20and%20perform%20only%20useful%20independent%20read-only%20inspection%3B%20do%20not%20launch%20the%20large%20roadmap%20automatically.%0D%0AUse%20the%20current%20versions%20of%20named%20input%20artifacts.%20If%20a%20required%20decision%2Finput%20is%20absent%2C%20record%20the%20precise%20blocker%20instead%20of%20inventing%20it%2C%20and%20complete%20useful%20independent%20inspection.%20No%20previous%20chat%20or%20another%20task%20prompt%20is%20required.%0D%0A%0D%0AHigh-level%20action%20plan%3A%0D%0A1.%20Freeze%20the%20release%20source%2Fpolicy%20revision%20and%20complete%20inventory%2C%20then%20evaluate%20every%20map%20and%20region%20under%20the%20final%20independent%20acceptance%20protocol.%0D%0A2.%20Audit%20A%2FB%20fixture%20motivation%2C%20shape%2Fpose%2Fcolor%2Fpower%2C%20global%2FC%20illumination%2C%20D%20fallback%2C%20unknown%20geometry%2C%20seams%2C%20shadows%20and%20major%20original%20color%2Fbrightness%20motifs.%0D%0A3.%20Verify%20canonical%2Fsidecar%20validation%2C%20selected%2Falternative%20configurations%2C%20asset%20hashes%2C%20missing-input%20behavior%2C%20round-trip%20replay%20and%20deterministic%20reruns.%0D%0A4.%20Publish%20per-map%20Complete%2FPartial%2FUnsupported%2FBlocked%20status%20and%20separate%20numerical%20execution%20coverage%20from%20accepted%20reconstruction%20coverage.%0D%0A5.%20Deliver%20a%20release%20manifest%2C%20machine-readable%20corpus%20summary%2C%20offline%20visual%20review%20index%2C%20reproduction%20instructions%2C%20known%20limitations%20and%20any%20explicitly%20remaining%20manual%20decisions.%0D%0A6.%20Mark%20the%20plan%20complete%20only%20if%20its%20frozen%20completion%20gates%20actually%20pass.%20Otherwise%20record%20a%20partial%20release%20and%20concrete%20remaining%20tasks.%0D%0A%0D%0ADefinition%20of%20Done%3A%0D%0A-%20Every%20original%20map%20has%20a%20reproducible%20saved%20result%20or%20a%20precisely%20reported%20non-success%20state%3B%20no%20map%20or%20unsupported%20area%20disappears.%0D%0A-%20All%20maps%20claimed%20fully%20relit%20meet%20the%20declared%20aesthetic%20and%20data-quality%20gates%20with%20the%20permitted%2C%20disclosed%20C%2FD%20budget.%0D%0A-%20The%20final%20data%20can%20be%20consumed%20later%20without%20changing%20the%20interpretation%20of%20existing%20assets%20or%20claiming%20historical%20source%20recovery.%0D%0A-%20The%20result%20ledger%20and%20plan%20accurately%20distinguish%20complete%20delivery%2C%20partial%20coverage%20and%20unresolved%20work.%0D%0A-%20Deliver%20the%20complete%20task%20artifact%2C%20appropriate%20verification%20evidence%20and%20the%20mandatory%20high-level%20plan-result%20append.%0D%0A%0D%0ANon-goals%20and%20things%20not%20to%20do%3A%0D%0ADo%20not%20ship%20GPU%2Fruntime%20consumers%2C%20modify%20world%20shading%2FBSP%20lighting%2C%20tackle%20double%20lighting%2C%20call%20renderer%20screenshots%20the%20data%20deliverable%2C%20or%20mark%20completion%20merely%20because%20all%20scripts%20exited%20successfully.%0D%0APrepare%20the%20concrete%20experiment%2Fdecision%20materials%2C%20run%20the%20authorized%20bounded%20evaluation%20if%20required%2C%20and%20issue%20a%20supported%20decision%20with%20alternatives%2C%20limitations%20and%20plan%20consequences.%20A%20negative%20result%20is%20valid%20evidence%3B%20do%20not%20manufacture%20a%20pass%20or%20leave%20only%20a%20proposal%20to%20investigate%20later.%0D%0A%0D%0ARequired%20completion%20and%20plan%20update%3A%0D%0AOpen%20docs%2Frend2-aesthetic-relighting-plan.md.%20Append%20a%20high-level%20result%20under%20%22AR-33%22%20in%20its%20Results%20Log%2C%20even%20when%20the%20outcome%20is%20Partial%2C%20Failed%20or%20Blocked.%20Include%20date%2Frevision%2C%20actual%20scope%2C%20what%20was%20learned%2Fimplemented%2C%20measured%20result%20and%20artifact%20paths%2Fhashes%2C%20A%2FB%2FC%2FD%20and%20coverage%20implications%2C%20assumptions%2Flimitations%2C%20decision%20and%20concrete%20next%20action.%20Update%20the%20task%27s%20status%20honestly.%20A%20successful%20build%2Fprocess%20is%20not%20aesthetic%20acceptance.%20Do%20not%20claim%20checks%20or%20measurements%20that%20were%20not%20performed.%20This%20is%20a%20decision%2Fexperiment%20task%3A%20you%20may%20revise%20the%20roadmap%2C%20dependencies%2C%20future%20prompts%20and%20add%20tasks%20when%20evidence%20justifies%20it.%20Record%20every%20structural%2Fpolicy%20change%20and%20its%20rationale%20in%20the%20plan%20change%20log.%20Preserve%20superseded%20tasks%2C%20failed%20results%20and%20frozen%20criteria%3B%20new%20tasks%20need%20self-contained%20English%20prompts%20and%20S%2FM%20sizing.%20A%20changed%20direction%20must%20have%20concrete%20deliverables%20and%20exit%20gates.%0D%0A)
